  `packages/mcp-server/test/note-version-tools.test.ts`:

  ```ts
  import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { EMPTY_NOTE_DOC } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import { noteTools } from '../src/tools/note-tools.js';
  import { eventBatches, recordingSession } from './fixtures/recording-session.js';

  const NOTE = 'N1';
  const WS = 'W1';
  const para = (text: string) => ({ type: 'doc', attrs: { schemaVersion: 2 }, content: [{ type: 'paragraph', content: [{ type: 'text', text }] }] });
  const OLD = para('old words');
  const NOW = para('current words');
  const versions = (...ids: string[]) => ({ versions: ids.map((eventId, i) => ({ eventId, authorId: 'U', recordedAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z', seq: i + 1 })) });
  const detail = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
    note: { noteId: NOTE, workspaceId: WS, homeProjectId: '', authorId: 'U', title: 'Cold room', scope: { mode: 'workspace' }, attachedTickets: [], deleted: false,
      createdAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z', updatedAt: '2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z', body: NOW, ...over },
  });
  const routes = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
    [`GET /v1/notes/${NOTE}`]: detail(),
    [`GET /v1/notes/${NOTE}/history`]: versions('V0', 'V1', 'V2'),
    [`GET /v1/notes/${NOTE}/history/V1`]: { body: OLD },
    ...over,
  });
  const restore = (session: ReturnType<typeof recordingSession>['session']) => {
    const found = noteTools(session).find((t) => t.name === 'restore_note_version');
    if (found === undefined) throw new Error('no restore_note_version');
    return found;
  };

  describe('restore_note_version (package)', () => {
    it('14.611 a versionId that is not a listed version of the note (a rename event\'s id, a stranger\'s) answers the uniform version sentence and writes nothing', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      for (const versionId of ['RENAMED-EVENT', 'NOT-A-THING']) {
        await expect(restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId })).rejects.toThrow('note version not found or not visible to you');
      }
      expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      expect(s.requests.some((r) => r.path.includes('/history/'))).toBe(false); // the body route is never asked for an unlisted id
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a listed version is restored.
      const out = await restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' });
      expect(out).toMatchObject({ noteId: NOTE, restoredFrom: 'V1', restored: true });
      expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('14.612 a listed version born without a body (the history-body route answers 404 for it) is restored as the empty document, not as an error', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes() }); // V0 is listed; GET …/history/V0 has no route, so it is a 404
      const out = await restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V0' });
      expect(out).toMatchObject({ restored: true, restoredFrom: 'V0' });
      // `toEqual` on the body, never `toMatchObject`: EMPTY_NOTE_DOC's `content: [{ type: 'paragraph' }]` is a SUBSET of every
      // one-paragraph document (OLD and NOW included), so a subset match passes whatever body was sent.
      expect(eventBatches(s.requests)[0]![0]).toMatchObject({ type: 'note.body_set' });
      expect((eventBatches(s.requests)[0]![0]!['payload'] as { body: unknown }).body).toEqual(EMPTY_NOTE_DOC);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a version WITH a body sends that body, not the empty one.
      const t = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      await restore(t.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' });
      expect((eventBatches(t.requests)[0]![0]!['payload'] as { body: unknown }).body).toEqual(OLD);
    });

    it('14.613 a version whose body equals the current one writes nothing and says so, whatever the key order', async () => {
      // The same document with its keys in another order: deep equality, not a string compare.
      const reordered = { content: [{ content: [{ text: 'old words', type: 'text' }], type: 'paragraph' }], attrs: { schemaVersion: 2 }, type: 'doc' };
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes({ [`GET /v1/notes/${NOTE}`]: detail({ body: reordered }) }) });
      expect(await restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' })).toEqual({
        noteId: NOTE, restoredFrom: 'V1', restored: false, reason: 'already the current body',
      });
      expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a different current body is replaced.
      const t = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      expect(await restore(t.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' })).toMatchObject({ restored: true });
    });

    it('14.614 a deleted note is refused before any write, with a sentence that names restore_note', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes({ [`GET /v1/notes/${NOTE}`]: detail({ deleted: true }) }) });
      await expect(restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' })).rejects.toThrow(/restore_note/);
      expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the same call on a live note writes.
      const t = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      await restore(t.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' });
      expect(eventBatches(t.requests)).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('14.616 a restore is exactly one note.body_set on the note\'s stream, origin mcp, actor the session user; the title, scope and filing are not touched', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      await restore(s.session).handler({ noteId: NOTE, versionId: 'V1' });
      const batches = eventBatches(s.requests);
      expect(batches).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(batches[0]).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(batches[0]![0]).toMatchObject({
        type: 'note.body_set', workspaceId: WS, entity: { kind: 'note', id: NOTE }, origin: 'mcp', actorId: '01J0A5ER0000000000000000AA', payload: { body: OLD },
      });
      expect(Object.keys((batches[0]![0] as { payload: object }).payload)).toEqual(['body']);
      expect(s.requests.filter((r) => r.method !== 'GET' && r.method !== 'EVENTS')).toEqual([]); // no other write of any kind
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.611: (a) drop the `listed.some(...)` check in `versionBody` → the unlisted ids reach `…/history/<id>`, 404, and restore `EMPTY_NOTE_DOC` (the call resolves); (b) fetch the body first and check the list after → the call still rejects but `…/history/RENAMED-EVENT` is in the request list; (c) map the list miss to `NOT_FOUND` instead of `VERSION_NOT_FOUND` → the sentence differs.
  - 14.612: (a) let the body route's 404 through (no `return EMPTY_NOTE_DOC`) → the V0 restore rejects; (b) answer the 404 by fetching the next listed version's body instead (V1, `OLD`) → `OLD` is a superset of `EMPTY_NOTE_DOC`, which the old `toMatchObject` leg accepted and the `toEqual` leg refuses.
  - 14.613: (a) compare with `body === note.body` → two parsed objects are never `===`, the equal body is written; (b) compare `JSON.stringify(body) === JSON.stringify(note.body)` → the reordered keys differ, the equal body is written; (c) drop the short-circuit → an event is sent.
  - 14.614: (a) drop `if (note.deleted) throw …` → the event is sent and the call resolves; (b) move the deleted check after `submitOne` → the call rejects but an event batch exists.
  - 14.616: (a) add `envelopeFor(…, 'note.renamed', { title: note.title })` and send both with `submitBatch` → the batch has two events; (b) put `{ body, title: note.title }` in the payload → the key list is `['body', 'title']`; (c) build the envelope with `newSubmittedEvent({ … actorId: note.authorId, origin: 'mcp' })` → `actorId` is `U`, not the session user.

  `apps/server/test/mcp/mcp-note-versions.test.ts` (hosted; 14.610, 14.612, 14.613, 14.615 and the live half of 14.616):

  ```ts
  import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { EMPTY_NOTE_DOC, newUlid } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from '../helpers/db.js';
  import { buildDoorWorld, callTool, type DoorWorld } from './helpers/door-matrix.js';
  import { event, eventCount, grantRole, mustCommit, noteBody } from './helpers/sweep2.js';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T4, cases 14.610, 14.612, 14.613, 14.615, 14.616 (the package half is `note-version-tools.test.ts`).
   * Every restore is a `tools/call` on `POST /mcp`; the versions are written as the app writes them. Run LOCALLY.
   */
  let db: TestDatabase;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDatabase();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db.drop();
  });

  const para = (text: string) => ({ type: 'doc', attrs: { schemaVersion: 2 }, content: [{ type: 'paragraph', content: [{ type: 'text', text }] }] });
  const cell = (type: string, text: string, attrs: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
    type, attrs: { colspan: 1, rowspan: 1, colwidth: null, ...attrs }, content: [{ type: 'paragraph', content: [{ type: 'text', text }] }],
  });
  /** A document Markdown cannot carry whole: a cell spanning two columns (`packages/editor/test/markdown-doc/convert.test.ts` 26.003 reports it as `table.span`). */
  const TABLE_SPAN = {
    type: 'doc', attrs: { schemaVersion: 2 },
    content: [{ type: 'table', content: [
      { type: 'tableRow', content: [cell('tableHeader', 'A'), cell('tableHeader', 'B')] },
      { type: 'tableRow', content: [cell('tableCell', '1'), cell('tableCell', '2', { colspan: 2 })] },
    ] }],
  };
  const NOT_YOURS = 'Only the note’s author, an admin who can read it, or the workspace owner can change it.'; // check-permission.ts, curly apostrophe

  type Version = { eventId: string };
  /** A workspace note by `who` with a born-empty first version, then one body_set per entry of `bodies`. Returns the note id and every version id, oldest first. */
  async function noteWithVersions(w: DoorWorld, who: DoorWorld['owner'], title: string, bodies: unknown[]) {
    const made = await callTool(w.app, who.token, 'create_note', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, title, scope: 'workspace' });
    expect(made.isError, made.text).toBe(false);
    const noteId = (made.json as { noteId: string }).noteId;
    for (const body of bodies) await mustCommit(w.app, who, [event(w.workspaceId, who, { kind: 'note', id: noteId }, 'note.body_set', { body })]);
    const history = await callTool(w.app, who.token, 'get_note_history', { noteId });
    return { noteId, ids: (history.json as { versions: Version[] }).versions.map((v) => v.eventId) };
  }

  describe('restore_note_version through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.610 the stored document comes back exactly, including what Markdown cannot carry; the update_note route through Markdown loses it', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2610');
      const { noteId, ids } = await noteWithVersions(w, w.owner, 'Lossy', [TABLE_SPAN, para('later')]);
      expect(ids).toHaveLength(3);
      // The precondition that makes the control mean something: Markdown reports the span as lost.
      const asMarkdown = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'get_note_history', { noteId, versionId: ids[1] });
      expect((asMarkdown.json as { lossy: string[] }).lossy).toEqual(['table.span']);
      const done = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[1] });
      expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
      expect(done.json).toMatchObject({ noteId, restoredFrom: ids[1], restored: true });
      expect(await noteBody(db, noteId)).toEqual(TABLE_SPAN);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: put the later body back, then write that version's MARKDOWN with update_note: the span is gone.
      await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[2] });
      expect(await noteBody(db, noteId)).toEqual(para('later')); // the starting point of the control
      const lossy = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'update_note', { noteId, markdown: (asMarkdown.json as { markdown: string }).markdown });
      expect(lossy.isError, lossy.text).toBe(false);
      // `not.toEqual(TABLE_SPAN)` alone held already at `para('later')`, so it passed with update_note a no-op. The Markdown
      // write must have LANDED (the body is a table now) and lost the span (no cell spans two columns).
      const viaMarkdown = JSON.stringify(await noteBody(db, noteId));
      expect(viaMarkdown).toContain('"type":"table"');
      expect(viaMarkdown).not.toContain('"colspan":2');
    });

    it('14.612 a version born without a body is listed, its body route answers 404, and the restore writes the empty document', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2612');
      const { noteId, ids } = await noteWithVersions(w, w.owner, 'Born empty', [para('first words')]);
      const direct = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'get_note_history', { noteId, versionId: ids[0] });
      expect(direct.isError).toBe(true); // the history-body route is a 404 for it (`read/notes.ts:369`)
      const done = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[0] });
      expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
      expect(await noteBody(db, noteId)).toEqual(EMPTY_NOTE_DOC);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the version with a body restores that body.
      await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[1] });
      expect(await noteBody(db, noteId)).toEqual(para('first words'));
    });

    it('14.613 restoring the current body writes nothing (the stored jsonb key order agrees); an older body is written and adds one event', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2613');
      // The keys are deliberately NOT in jsonb's canonical order.
      const current = { content: [{ content: [{ text: 'same words', type: 'text' }], type: 'paragraph' }], attrs: { schemaVersion: 2 }, type: 'doc' };
      const { noteId, ids } = await noteWithVersions(w, w.owner, 'Same', [current]);
      const before = await eventCount(db, noteId);
      const same = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[1] });
      expect(same.json).toEqual({ noteId, restoredFrom: ids[1], restored: false, reason: 'already the current body' });
      expect(await eventCount(db, noteId)).toBe(before);
      const older = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId, versionId: ids[0] });
      expect(older.json).toMatchObject({ restored: true });
      expect(await eventCount(db, noteId)).toBe(before + 1); // CONTROL: exactly one event (14.616, live)
    });

    it('14.615 the role matrix: the author, an admin who can read it and the owner restore; a plain member who is not the author is refused in the gate\'s own words and nothing is written', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2615');
      const ownerNote = await noteWithVersions(w, w.owner, 'Owner note', [para('A'), para('B')]);
      const memberNote = await noteWithVersions(w, w.member, 'Member note', [para('X'), para('Y')]);
      const as = (who: DoorWorld['owner'], n: { noteId: string; ids: string[] }, version: number) =>
        callTool(w.app, who.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId: n.noteId, versionId: n.ids[version] });
      const refused = await as(w.member, ownerNote, 1);
      expect(refused.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(refused.text).toBe(`refused by permission.note: ${NOT_YOURS}`); // verbatim, with no "event 0" prefix
      expect(await noteBody(db, ownerNote.noteId)).toEqual(para('B')); // nothing written
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the author restores their own note.
      expect((await as(w.member, memberNote, 1)).isError).toBe(false);
      expect(await noteBody(db, memberNote.noteId)).toEqual(para('X'));
      // An admin who can read it.
      await grantRole(w.app, w.owner, w.workspaceId, w.member.userId, 'admin');
      expect((await as(w.member, ownerNote, 1)).isError).toBe(false);
      expect(await noteBody(db, ownerNote.noteId)).toEqual(para('A'));
      // The owner, on a note the member wrote.
      expect((await as(w.owner, memberNote, 2)).isError).toBe(false);
      expect(await noteBody(db, memberNote.noteId)).toEqual(para('Y'));
      // A stranger and an unknown note get the uniform sentence.
      const stranger = await callTool(w.app, w.stranger.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId: ownerNote.noteId, versionId: ownerNote.ids[1] });
      expect(stranger.text).toBe('note not found or not visible to you');
      // The comment above names an unknown note too: assert it (the owner, a note id nobody minted).
      const unknown = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'restore_note_version', { noteId: newUlid(), versionId: ownerNote.ids[1] });
      expect(unknown.text).toBe('note not found or not visible to you');
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.610: (a) restore through `markdownBody(session, ws, bodyAsMarkdown(body).markdown)` (the `update_note` path) → the stored body loses the span and is not `TABLE_SPAN`; (b) `versionBody` reads `get_note_history`'s Markdown answer and converts it back → same; (c) the tool writes nothing and answers `restored: true` → the body stays `para('later')`.
  - 14.612 (hosted): (a) let the body route's 404 through → `done.isError` is true; (b) answer the 404 with `null` (the route's own "no body") → the stored body is not `EMPTY_NOTE_DOC`.
  - 14.613 (hosted): (a) compare with `===` → the same body is written and the event count moves; (b) drop the short-circuit → same.
  - 14.615: (a) send the restore with `submitBatch` instead of `submitOne` → the member's refusal reads `event 0 refused by permission.note: …`; (b) map a `refused` result to `{ restored: false, reason }` → `refused.isError` is false; (c) `readNote`'s 404 mapping is bypassed (call `session.get` directly) → the stranger's text is `gisik-nokma answered 404`. The role matrix itself (who may) is the gate's: no edit of this task's code can grant authority, and the case line says so.

  After `readNote`:

  ```ts
  /**
   * The stored body of one LISTED version of a note, copied and never converted (MCP sweep 2 §4.1). The version must be in
   * the note's own history list (RV-2: an id of a rename event is not one); the body route answers 404 for a version born
   * without a body (`read/notes.ts:369`), and the document the reducer folds in for that version is the empty one
   * (`note-state.ts:139`), so that is what is restored (RV-3).
   */
  async function versionBody(session: Session, noteId: string, versionId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    let listed: Array<{ eventId: string }>;
    try {
      listed = ((await session.get(`/v1/notes/${enc(noteId)}/history`)) as { versions: Array<{ eventId: string }> }).versions;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError && error.status === 404) throw new Error(NOT_FOUND);
      throw error;
    }
    if (!listed.some((version) => version.eventId === versionId)) throw new Error(VERSION_NOT_FOUND);
    try {
      return ((await session.get(`/v1/notes/${enc(noteId)}/history/${enc(versionId)}`)) as { body: unknown }).body;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError && error.status === 404) return EMPTY_NOTE_DOC;
      throw error;
    }
  }
  ```

  In `get_note_history` change line 205's literal to `VERSION_NOT_FOUND` (same text). Between `restore_note` and `get_note_history` add:

  ```ts
    {
      name: 'restore_note_version',
      description:
        'Put an earlier saved version of a note back as its current body (versionId from get_note_history). The stored document is copied exactly, so ' +
        'nothing that Markdown cannot carry is lost; the version it replaces stays in the history, so a restore is undone by restoring that version. ' +
        'Only the note\'s author, an admin who can read it, or the owner can. A deleted note must be brought back with restore_note first. ' +
        'Mentions that the restored text adds notify those people.',
      inputSchema: { noteId: id, versionId: id },
      handler: async (args) => {
        const a = z.object({ noteId: id, versionId: id }).parse(args);
        const note = await readNote(session, a.noteId);
        if (note.deleted) throw new Error(DELETED_NOTE);
        const body = await versionBody(session, a.noteId, a.versionId);
        // Both sides are jsonb, so their key order agrees; `isDeepStrictEqual` does not rely on it.
        if (isDeepStrictEqual(body, note.body)) return { noteId: a.noteId, restoredFrom: a.versionId, restored: false, reason: 'already the current body' };
        const committed = await submitOne(session, envelopeFor(session, note.workspaceId, { kind: 'note', id: a.noteId }, 'note.body_set', { body }));
        return { noteId: a.noteId, restoredFrom: a.versionId, restored: true, ...(committed as object) };
      },
    },
  ```

  ```ts
    if (found === undefined) throw new Error('no close_tickets');
    return found;

      expect(dup.requests, 'the duplicate refusal reads nothing').toEqual([]);

      const s = recordingSession();

    });

      const s = recordingSession({
        events: (events) => ({ results: events.map((_, i) => ({ status: 'committed', eventId: String(events[(i + 1) % events.length]!['id']), seq: i + 1, serverTime: 't' })) }),
      });
      const out = (await closeWith(s.session, ['T1', 'T2', 'T3'], { confirm_count: '3' })) as { results: Array<{ ticketId: string; eventId: string }> };
      const sentIds = eventBatches(s.requests)[0]!.map((e) => String(e['id']));
      expect(out.results.map((r) => r.ticketId)).toEqual(['T1', 'T2', 'T3']);
      expect(out.results.map((r) => r.eventId)).toEqual([sentIds[1], sentIds[2], sentIds[0]]); // the answer's own ids, in the answer's own order
    });

    it('14.624 without the exact count (none, "2", "03", "3 ", 3.5, 4) the tool answers the preview and sends NOTHING; the exact count, as a string or a number, closes', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes() });
      for (const wrong of [undefined, '2', '03', '3 ', 3.5, 4, '']) {
        const reply = (await closeWith(s.session, ['T1', 'T2', 'T3'], wrong === undefined ? {} : { confirm_count: wrong })) as { preview?: boolean; confirm?: string };
        expect(reply.preview, String(wrong)).toBe(true);

    it('14.625 the preview names every ticket by key and title, lists an unreadable id in the same words whether it is hidden or missing, counts the tickets and says the server decides each close', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: routes() }); // T3 has no route: a 404, which is what hidden AND missing both look like
      const reply = (await closeWith(s.session, ['T1', 'T2', 'T3'])) as { preview: boolean; wouldChange: string; counts: Record<string, number>; nothingChanged: boolean; confirm: string };
      expect(reply.preview).toBe(true);
      expect(reply.wouldChange).toContain('Closes 3 tickets: CCSO-4 "Fix login", CCSO-9 "Rate limits" and one you cannot see (T3).');

  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.617: (a) `ticketIds: z.array(id).min(1).max(101)` → the `101` row closes; (b) delete the `repeated` check → the `a duplicate` row closes; (c) move the `repeated` check below `confirmOrPreview` → the no-count duplicate answers a preview (three reads) instead of the refusal; (d) `z.array(z.string())` (drop `.min(1)` on the id) → the `an empty id` row closes.
  - 14.618: (a) `[...a.ticketIds].sort().map(…)` when building `events` → the entity ids are `['T1', 'T2', 'T3']`; (b) build the confirmed path's events after the preview's `readTicket` loop (always read) → the GET list is not empty; (c) send `ticket.closed` with `{ comment: … }` or any non-empty payload → `toEqual({})` fails.
  - 14.619: (a) `submitBatch` instead of `submitBatchByIndex` → the call throws; (b) `refused: rows.filter((r) => r.status === 'refused').length` → the invalid batch counts 0 refused; (c) `closeRow` maps `invalid` to `status: 'refused'` → the invalid row differs.
  - 14.622: (a) wrap `submitBatchByIndex` in a `try` whose `catch` answers `{ closed: count, refused: 0, results: [] }` → every row resolves; (b) `submitBatchByIndex` called with a `whenUnknown` that does not name `list_tickets` → the regex fails.
  - 14.623 (package): (a) match each ticket to its result by event id (`results.find((r) => r.eventId === events[i].id)`) → the eventId leg reads `[s0, s1, s2]`; (b) map results in the ANSWER's order to tickets by their event id → the ticketId leg reads `['T2', 'T3', 'T1']`.
  - 14.624: (a) `Number(a.confirm_count) === count` → `'03'` and `'3 '` close; (b) `parseInt` → `3.5` closes; (c) submit before `confirmOrPreview` → `sent(...)` is not empty.
  - 14.625: (a) drop the `TICKET_NOT_FOUND` branch → T3's 404 escapes and the call rejects; (b) always `tickets` (no singular) → the one-ticket control fails; (c) `counts: {}` → `toEqual({ tickets: 3 })` fails.

  `apps/server/test/mcp/mcp-close-tickets.test.ts` (hosted; the server decides, nothing is stubbed):

  ```ts
  import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { newUlid } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import { TICKET_NOT_VISIBLE_REASON } from '../../src/permissions/check-permission.js';
  import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from '../helpers/db.js';
  import { buildDoorWorld, callTool, type DoorWorld } from './helpers/door-matrix.js';
  import { event, eventCount, mustCommit } from './helpers/sweep2.js';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T5, cases 14.619 (server half), 14.620, 14.621, 14.623 (history half), 14.626 (the package half of the rest is
   * `close-tickets.test.ts`). Every call is a `tools/call` on `POST /mcp`. Run LOCALLY.
   */
  let db: TestDatabase;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDatabase();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db.drop();
  });

  type Row = { ticketId: string; status: string; rule?: string; reason?: string };
  type Closed = { closed: number; refused: number; results: Row[] };

  /** A ticket born in focus; `terminal` walks it doing, done (the 14.457 route: the workflow decides, so reach a terminal state first). */
  async function ticket(w: DoorWorld, title: string, opts: { terminal?: boolean; typeId?: string; parentId?: string } = {}): Promise<string> {
    const made = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'create_ticket', {
      workspaceId: w.workspaceId, projectId: w.projectId, typeId: opts.typeId ?? 'task', title, start: 'focus', ...(opts.parentId === undefined ? {} : { parentId: opts.parentId }),
    });
    expect(made.isError, made.text).toBe(false);
    const ticketId = (made.json as { ticketId: string }).ticketId;
    if (opts.terminal === true) {
      for (const to of ['doing', 'done']) {
        const moved = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'transition_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId, to });
        expect(moved.isError, `${title} -> ${to}: ${moved.text}`).toBe(false);
      }
    }
    return ticketId;
  }
  const closeMany = (w: DoorWorld, who: DoorWorld['owner'], ticketIds: string[], confirm = true) =>
    callTool(w.app, who.token, 'close_tickets', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketIds, ...(confirm ? { confirm_count: String(ticketIds.length) } : {}) });

  describe('close_tickets through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.619 three tickets, the middle one not terminal: the first and third ARE closed on the server, the middle is refused with the engine\'s own words, and close_ticket on it refuses with the same', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2619');
      const [a, b, c] = [await ticket(w, 'A', { terminal: true }), await ticket(w, 'B'), await ticket(w, 'C', { terminal: true })];
      const out = await closeMany(w, w.owner, [a, b, c]);
      expect(out.isError, out.text).toBe(false);
      const reply = out.json as Closed;
      expect(reply).toMatchObject({ closed: 2, refused: 1 });
      expect(reply.results.map((r) => [r.ticketId, r.status])).toEqual([[a, 'closed'], [b, 'refused'], [c, 'closed']]);
      expect(reply.results[1]).toMatchObject({ rule: 'close-requires-terminal' });
      expect(reply.results[1]!.reason).toMatch(/^A ticket can only be closed from a terminal state; ".+" is not one\.$/);
      expect([await eventCount(db, a, 'ticket.closed'), await eventCount(db, b, 'ticket.closed'), await eventCount(db, c, 'ticket.closed')]).toEqual([1, 0, 1]);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the single close refuses with the same rule and sentence.
      const single = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'close_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: b });
      expect(single.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(single.text).toBe(`event 0 refused by ${reply.results[1]!.rule}: ${reply.results[1]!.reason}`);
    });

    it('14.620 a parent and its terminal children all close, in either order (the engine reads the children\'s state, not their closed flag)', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2620');
      for (const parentFirst of [true, false]) {
        const parent = await ticket(w, `Epic ${parentFirst}`, { typeId: 'epic' });
        const kids = [await ticket(w, 'Kid one', { terminal: true, parentId: parent }), await ticket(w, 'Kid two', { terminal: true, parentId: parent })];
        for (const to of ['doing', 'done']) expect((await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'transition_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: parent, to })).isError).toBe(false);
        const order = parentFirst ? [parent, ...kids] : [...kids, parent];
        const reply = (await closeMany(w, w.owner, order)).json as Closed;
        expect(reply, `parentFirst=${parentFirst}: ${JSON.stringify(reply)}`).toMatchObject({ closed: 3, refused: 0 });
      }
    });

    it('14.621 a ticket the caller cannot see and one that does not exist get the SAME refusal; a visible terminal ticket in the same batch closes', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2621');
      const hidden = await ticket(w, 'Salary review', { terminal: true });
      await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'ticket', id: hidden }, 'visibility.set', { ticketId: hidden, mode: 'explicit', userIds: [w.owner.userId] })]);
      const visible = await ticket(w, 'Visible', { terminal: true });
      const ghost = newUlid();
      const asMember = (await closeMany(w, w.member, [hidden, ghost])).json as Closed;
      expect(asMember).toMatchObject({ closed: 0, refused: 2 });
      expect(asMember.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'refused', reason: TICKET_NOT_VISIBLE_REASON });
      expect({ rule: asMember.results[1]!.rule, reason: asMember.results[1]!.reason }).toEqual({ rule: asMember.results[0]!.rule, reason: asMember.results[0]!.reason });
      expect(await eventCount(db, hidden, 'ticket.closed')).toBe(0);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the owner can see and close both the hidden and the visible one.
      expect(((await closeMany(w, w.owner, [hidden, visible])).json as Closed)).toMatchObject({ closed: 2 });
    });

    it('14.623 a closed ticket\'s History shows the close as the assistant\'s (origin mcp) and reopen_ticket undoes it', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2623');
      const a = await ticket(w, 'History', { terminal: true });
      expect(((await closeMany(w, w.owner, [a])).json as Closed).closed).toBe(1);
      const history = async () => ((await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'get_ticket_history', { ticketId: a })).json as { events: Array<{ type: string; origin: string }> }).events;
      expect((await history()).find((e) => e.type === 'ticket.closed')).toMatchObject({ origin: 'mcp' });
      const back = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'reopen_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: a });
      expect(back.isError, back.text).toBe(false);
      expect((await history()).map((e) => e.type)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['ticket.closed', 'ticket.reopened']));
    });

    it('14.626 the preview is not a promise: a non-terminal ticket is in the preview and is then refused at the close with the engine\'s sentence', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2626');
      const [done, open] = [await ticket(w, 'Done one', { terminal: true }), await ticket(w, 'Still open')];
      const preview = (await closeMany(w, w.owner, [done, open], false)).json as { preview: boolean; wouldChange: string; nothingChanged: boolean };
      expect(preview).toMatchObject({ preview: true, nothingChanged: true });
      expect(preview.wouldChange).toContain('"Still open"'); // listed like any other
      expect(await eventCount(db, open, 'ticket.closed')).toBe(0);
      // The terminal one is the leg that can fail: a preview that wrote would CLOSE it (the open one is refused either way).
      expect(await eventCount(db, done, 'ticket.closed')).toBe(0);
      const closed = (await closeMany(w, w.owner, [done, open])).json as Closed;
      expect(closed.results[1]).toMatchObject({ status: 'refused', rule: 'close-requires-terminal' });
      expect(closed.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'closed' }); // CONTROL: the terminal one closed
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.619 (hosted): (a) `submitBatch` instead of `submitBatchByIndex` → `out.isError` is true while the ledger shows two closes; (b) `closeRow` drops `reason` → the reason regex fails.
  - 14.620: none, guards existing behaviour (the engine's `close-requires-terminal` reads the children's state category, not their closed flag). Non-vacuous: `closed: 3, refused: 0` fails if the engine refuses either order.
  - 14.621: none of this task's code can make a hidden ticket close; it guards the gate's no-existence-oracle rule. Edit that it does catch: (a) the tool reads each ticket before a CONFIRMED close and drops the unreadable ones → `refused: 2` becomes `0` rows and `toMatchObject({ refused: 2 })` fails.
  - 14.623 (hosted): (a) `envelopeFor` replaced in `close_tickets` by an envelope with `origin: 'web'` → the History line's origin is `web`.
  - 14.626: (a) submit before `confirmOrPreview` → the terminal ticket is closed by the preview call, and `eventCount(done)` is 1.

  and `readTicket`'s throw uses it (`throw new Error(TICKET_NOT_FOUND)`). Below `historyLine`, before `ticketTools`:

  ```ts
  const closeManyShape = {
    workspaceId: id,
    ticketIds: z.array(id).min(1).max(100),
    // A string or a number, and NOT `.int()`: 3.5 must reach the preview, not a schema error (CT-9).
    confirm_count: z.union([z.string(), z.number()]).optional(),
  };
  const CLOSE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN =
    'gisik-nokma did not report what happened to every ticket, so which ones closed is unknown. Do not assume any closed or any left open; read them again with list_tickets before doing anything else';

  /** "a, b and c"; "a"; "". */
  function inWords(items: string[]): string {
    return items.length < 2 ? (items[0] ?? '') : `${items.slice(0, -1).join(', ')} and ${items[items.length - 1]}`;
  }

  function closeRow(ticketId: string, r: EventResult) {
    if (r.status === 'committed') return { ticketId, status: 'closed' as const, eventId: r.eventId, seq: r.seq };
    if (r.status === 'refused') return { ticketId, status: 'refused' as const, rule: r.rule, reason: r.reason };
    return { ticketId, status: 'invalid' as const, code: r.code, message: r.message };
  }
  ```

  The tool, right after the `...(['close', 'reopen'] as const).map(...)` spread:

  ```ts
      {
        name: 'close_tickets',
        description:
          'Close several tickets at once (1-100 ticketIds), as the List view\'s Bulk close does: one close per ticket, each decided by the product\'s own rules ' +
          '(a ticket closes only from a terminal state, and not while it has open children). Two calls: without confirm_count you get a preview that names every ' +
          'ticket and nothing changes; repeat the call with confirm_count set to the number of tickets to close them. The answer lists every ticket as closed or ' +
          'refused (with the product\'s reason); a refused ticket stays open and the rest are still closed. Undo with reopen_ticket.',
        inputSchema: closeManyShape,
        handler: async (args) => {
          const a = z.object(closeManyShape).parse(args);
          const repeated = a.ticketIds.find((ticketId, i) => a.ticketIds.indexOf(ticketId) !== i);
          if (repeated !== undefined) throw new Error(`ticketIds lists ${repeated} more than once; list each ticket once`);
          const count = a.ticketIds.length;
          // The count binds the SIZE of the batch, not its members: a different list of the same length is a new batch the caller chose (spec §5.7).
          const preview = await confirmOrPreview(String(count), a.confirm_count === undefined ? undefined : String(a.confirm_count), async () => {
            const named: string[] = [];
            for (const ticketId of a.ticketIds) {
              try {
                const ticket = await readTicket(session, a.workspaceId, ticketId);
                named.push(`${ticket.key ?? ticketId} "${ticket.title}"`);
              } catch (error) {
                // Hidden and missing look the same on purpose (no existence oracle).
                if (error instanceof Error && error.message === TICKET_NOT_FOUND) named.push(`one you cannot see (${ticketId})`);
                else throw error;
              }
            }
            return {
              sentence: `Closes ${count} ticket${count === 1 ? '' : 's'}: ${inWords(named)}. The server decides each close; a ticket that is not in a terminal state, or has open children, will be refused and left open.`,
              counts: { tickets: count },
            };
          }, 'confirm_count');
          if (preview !== null) return preview;
          const events = a.ticketIds.map((ticketId) => envelopeFor(session, a.workspaceId, { kind: 'ticket', id: ticketId }, 'ticket.closed', {}));
          const results = await submitBatchByIndex(session, events, CLOSE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN);
          const rows = a.ticketIds.map((ticketId, i) => closeRow(ticketId, results[i]!));
          return { closed: rows.filter((r) => r.status === 'closed').length, refused: rows.filter((r) => r.status !== 'closed').length, results: rows };
        },
      },
  ```

  ```ts
      expect(stored).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['mention'], changed: true });
      expect(puts(s.requests)).toEqual([{ method: 'PUT', path: PREFS, body: { mutedKinds: ['mention'] } }]);
      expect(await get(s.session)).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['mention'] });
      // The stdio door: createSession over a REAL client against a stub server (`Session.put` for real, NP-8).
      let doc = { mutedKinds: [] as string[] };
      const methods: string[] = [];
      const stub: typeof fetch = async (input, init) => {
        const req = new Request(input as string, init);
        methods.push(`${req.method} ${new URL(req.url).pathname}`);
        if (new URL(req.url).pathname !== PREFS) return new Response('{}', { status: 404 });
        if (req.method === 'PUT') doc = JSON.parse(await req.text()) as typeof doc;
        return new Response(JSON.stringify(doc), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      };
      const tokenPath = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'gisik-np-')), 'mcp-token');
      await writeCachedToken(tokenPath, { token: 't', userId: 'U' });
      const real = await createSession({
        config: { serverUrl: 'http://server', tokenPath, pollIntervalMs: DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL_MS },
        client: createGisikClient('http://server', stub), prompt: () => {}, sleep: async () => {}, now: () => 0,
      });
      expect(await set(real, { mute: ['assignment'] })).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['assignment'], changed: true });
      expect((await get(real) as { mutedKinds: string[] }).mutedKinds).toEqual(['assignment']);
      expect(methods).toEqual([`GET ${PREFS}`, `PUT ${PREFS}`, `GET ${PREFS}`]);
    });

    it('14.629 read-modify-write: mute adds, unmute removes, nothing is duplicated, and a call that changes nothing sends no PUT', async () => {
      const s = stateful(['mention']);
      expect(await set(s.session, { mute: ['assignment'] })).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['mention', 'assignment'], changed: true });
      expect(await set(s.session, { unmute: ['mention'] })).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['assignment'], changed: true });
      expect(puts(s.requests).map((p) => (p.body as { mutedKinds: string[] }).mutedKinds)).toEqual([['mention', 'assignment'], ['assignment']]);
      // No change: already muted, and unmuting what is not muted.
      expect(await set(s.session, { mute: ['assignment'] })).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['assignment'], changed: false });
      expect(await set(s.session, { unmute: ['stale-ticket'] })).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['assignment'], changed: false });
      expect(puts(s.requests)).toHaveLength(2);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a repeated kind in one call is stored once.
      expect(await set(s.session, { mute: ['stale-ticket', 'stale-ticket'] })).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['assignment', 'stale-ticket'], changed: true });
    });

    it('14.630 both lists empty or absent, a kind in both, and an unknown kind are refused before any request', async () => {
      for (const [label, args] of [
        ['nothing', {}], ['both empty', { mute: [], unmute: [] }], ['a kind in both', { mute: ['mention'], unmute: ['mention'] }], ['an unknown kind', { mute: ['bogus'] }],
      ] as const) {
        const s = stateful([]);
        await expect(set(s.session, { ...args }), label).rejects.toThrow();
        expect(s.requests, `${label}: no request, not even the read`).toEqual([]);
      }
      // CONTROL, same `it`: one kind goes through.
      const ok = stateful([]);
      expect(await set(ok.session, { unmute: ['mention'], mute: ['assignment'] })).toMatchObject({ changed: true });
    });

    it('14.632 both descriptions name every member of NOTIFICATION_KINDS and the app\'s label for mention, so a kind added later fails here', () => {
      const { session } = recordingSession();
      const missing = (description: string): string[] => NOTIFICATION_KINDS.filter((kind) => !description.includes(kind));
      for (const name of ['get_notification_preferences', 'set_notification_preferences']) {
        const description = find(session, name).description;
        expect(missing(description), `${name} is missing kinds`).toEqual([]);
        expect(description).toContain('You are mentioned in a comment, description or note'); // the app's label for mention
        // The kind NAMES come from NOTIFICATION_KINDS itself, so `missing()` stays green when a kind has no words: KINDS_IN_WORDS then
        // prints `kind (undefined)`. This leg is what turns "a kind added later" red at RUN time, not only at typecheck.
        expect(description).not.toContain('(undefined)');
      }
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the check can see a missing kind.
      expect(missing('mention only')).toEqual(NOTIFICATION_KINDS.filter((kind) => kind !== 'mention'));
    });

    it('14.633 the tools take no user: get has no arguments, set has only mute and unmute', () => {
      const { session } = recordingSession();
      expect(Object.keys(find(session, 'get_notification_preferences').inputSchema)).toEqual([]);
      expect(Object.keys(find(session, 'set_notification_preferences').inputSchema).sort()).toEqual(['mute', 'unmute']);
      expect(Object.keys(find(session, 'list_saved_filters').inputSchema)).toEqual(['workspaceId']);
    });
  });

  describe('list_saved_filters (package)', () => {
    // Stored order is f2 then f1 on purpose: sorted by id ('f1' < 'f2') or by name ('Mine, open' < 'P1s') both reverse it, so
    // "in stored order" can fail.
    const doc = {
      savedFilters: [
        { id: 'f2', name: 'P1s', filterSpec: { priority: 'P1' } },
        { id: 'f1', name: 'Mine, open', filterSpec: { assigneeId: 'U1', closed: false } },
      ],
      listColumns: { P1: ['status'] }, grouping: {},
    };
    const list = (s: Session) => find(s, 'list_saved_filters').handler({ workspaceId: 'W1' });

    it('14.634 lists the caller\'s saved filters for the workspace in stored order as { id, name, filter }, nothing else of the document; none saved is []; it only reads', async () => {
      const s = recordingSession({ routes: { 'GET /v1/me/preferences': doc } });
      expect(await list(s.session)).toEqual({
        workspaceId: 'W1',
        savedFilters: [
          { id: 'f2', name: 'P1s', filter: { priority: 'P1' } },
          { id: 'f1', name: 'Mine, open', filter: { assigneeId: 'U1', closed: false } },
        ],
      });
      expect(s.requests).toEqual([{ method: 'GET', path: '/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=W1' }]);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: nothing saved is an empty list, and listColumns / grouping never leak.
      const empty = recordingSession({ routes: { 'GET /v1/me/preferences': { savedFilters: [], listColumns: {}, grouping: {} } } });
      expect(await list(empty.session)).toEqual({ workspaceId: 'W1', savedFilters: [] });
      expect(JSON.stringify(await list(s.session))).not.toContain('listColumns');
    });

    it('14.637 a workspace the caller cannot see answers the uniform sentence, and the tool never writes', async () => {
      const gone = recordingSession(); // no route: a 404
      await expect(list(gone.session)).rejects.toThrow('workspace not found or not visible to you');
      // `every` over an empty list is true: pin the one read first, then that nothing else was sent.
      expect(gone.requests).toEqual([{ method: 'GET', path: '/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=W1' }]);
      const ok = recordingSession({ routes: { 'GET /v1/me/preferences': doc } });
      await list(ok.session);
      expect(ok.requests).toEqual([{ method: 'GET', path: '/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=W1' }]); // CONTROL: nor does a success
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.627: (a) `readMuted` answers `[]` when `safeParse` fails → the `{ nope: 1 }` case resolves; (b) `kinds` built from `[...NOTIFICATION_KINDS].sort()` → `contested-claim` moves ahead of `mention` and the order leg fails; (c) `muted: mutedKinds.length > 0` → the control shows five muted, not `['mention']`.
  - 14.628: (a) `putJson` → `postJson` (the verb is POST) → the stdio stub keeps `[]` and the reply is `{ mutedKinds: [], changed: true }`, and `methods` reads `POST`; (b) answer `{ mutedKinds: kept, changed: true }` without the PUT → `puts(...)` is empty.
  - 14.629: (a) PUT `{ mutedKinds: mute }` (the named kinds, not the merged document) → the first PUT body is `['assignment']`; (b) drop the `if (!changed)` short-circuit → the PUT count is 4; (c) push `mute` without the `includes` guard → the control stores `stale-ticket` twice.
  - 14.630: (a) call `readMuted` before the two refusals → the request list holds the GET; (b) drop the `both` check → `a kind in both` sends a PUT.
  - 14.632: (a) drop a kind from `KIND_WORDS` (typed as `Partial<Record<…>>` to get past the compiler) → the description reads `kind (undefined)`; (b) build `KINDS_IN_WORDS` from `Object.keys(KIND_WORDS)` with a kind missing → `missing()` names it; (c) `set_notification_preferences`'s description drops `${KINDS_IN_WORDS}` → `missing()` names all five.
  - 14.633 (package): (a) add `userId: z.string().optional()` to `muteShape` → the key list is `['mute', 'unmute', 'userId']`; (b) give `get_notification_preferences` an input schema → its key list is not empty.
  - 14.634: (a) return `parsed.data` whole → `listColumns` leaks; (b) sort the saved filters by name or by id → the stored order `f2, f1` is lost; (c) keep the key `filterSpec` → the equality fails; (d) query `/v1/me/preferences` without `?workspaceId=` → the request list differs.
  - 14.637 (package): (a) catch the 404 and answer `{ workspaceId, savedFilters: [] }` → the refusal case resolves; (b) call `session.get` directly instead of `readOrNotFound` → the text is `gisik-nokma answered 404`.

  ```ts
  import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
  import { filterSpecSchema } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import { buildOAuthApp, hostedToken } from '../helpers/oauth.js';
  import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from '../helpers/db.js';
  import { signInWithPhone } from '../helpers/session.js';
  import { buildDoorWorld, callTool, type DoorWorld } from './helpers/door-matrix.js';
  import { grantRole } from './helpers/sweep2.js';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T6, cases 14.628 (hosted half), 14.631, 14.633, 14.635, 14.636, 14.637 (the package half is
   * `notification-preferences.test.ts`). Every call is a `tools/call` on `POST /mcp`. Run LOCALLY.
   */
  let db: TestDatabase;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDatabase();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db.drop();
  });

  type Prefs = { mutedKinds: string[]; changed?: boolean };
  const route = (w: DoorWorld, who: DoorWorld['owner'], method: 'GET' | 'PUT', url: string, payload?: unknown) =>
    w.app.inject({ method, url, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${who.sessionToken}` }, ...(payload === undefined ? {} : { payload: payload as Record<string, unknown> }) });
  const saveFilters = (w: DoorWorld, who: DoorWorld['owner'], savedFilters: unknown[]) =>
    route(w, who, 'PUT', `/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=${w.workspaceId}`, { savedFilters, listColumns: {}, grouping: {} });

  describe('notification preferences through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.628 set_notification_preferences reaches PUT /v1/me/notification-preferences on the hosted door; the stored document is what the app\'s own route reads', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2628');
      const done = await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'set_notification_preferences', { mute: ['mention', 'stale-ticket'] });
      expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
      expect(done.json).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['mention', 'stale-ticket'], changed: true });
      expect((await route(w, w.member, 'GET', '/v1/me/notification-preferences')).json()).toEqual({ mutedKinds: ['mention', 'stale-ticket'] });
      expect((await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'get_notification_preferences', {})).json).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['mention', 'stale-ticket'] }); // CONTROL: the read tool agrees
    });

    it('14.631 muting mention hides mention rows from list_notifications and the unread count, unmuting brings them back, and the rows were never deleted', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2631');
      const mention = `@[${w.memberName}](user:${w.member.userId})`;
      const added = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'add_comment', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: w.ticketId, markdown: `ping ${mention}` });
      expect(added.isError, added.text).toBe(false);
      const kinds = async () => ((await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'list_notifications', {})).json as { notifications: Array<{ kind: string }> }).notifications.map((n) => n.kind);
      const unread = async () => (await route(w, w.member, 'GET', '/v1/me/notifications/unread-count')).json<{ unread: number }>().unread;
      const rows = async () => (await db.pool.query<{ n: number }>(`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM notifications WHERE user_id = $1 AND kind = 'mention'`, [w.member.userId])).rows[0]!.n;
      await vi.waitFor(async () => expect(await kinds()).toContain('mention'), { timeout: 5000, interval: 50 }); // the fan-out runs after the commit
      const before = await unread();
      expect(before).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'set_notification_preferences', { mute: ['mention'] });
      expect(await kinds()).not.toContain('mention');
      expect(await unread()).toBe(before - (await rows()));
      expect(await rows()).toBeGreaterThan(0); // hidden, never deleted
      await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'set_notification_preferences', { unmute: ['mention'] });
      expect(await kinds()).toContain('mention');
      expect(await unread()).toBe(before);
    });

    it('14.633 isolation: one person\'s set leaves another person\'s document unchanged', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2633');
      await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'set_notification_preferences', { mute: ['assignment'] });
      expect((await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'get_notification_preferences', {})).json).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: [] });
      expect((await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'get_notification_preferences', {})).json).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['assignment'] }); // CONTROL
    });
  });

  describe('list_saved_filters through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.635 a saved filter comes back unchanged, is accepted by filterSpecSchema, and list_tickets takes it as its filter', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2635');
      const assigned = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'assign_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: w.ticketId, assigneeId: w.member.userId });
      expect(assigned.isError, assigned.text).toBe(false);
      const spec = { assigneeId: w.member.userId, closed: false };
      expect((await saveFilters(w, w.member, [{ id: 'f1', name: 'Mine, open', filterSpec: spec }])).statusCode).toBe(200);
      const out = await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'list_saved_filters', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId });
      expect(out.isError, out.text).toBe(false);
      const filter = (out.json as { savedFilters: Array<{ id: string; name: string; filter: unknown }> }).savedFilters[0]!;
      expect(filter).toEqual({ id: 'f1', name: 'Mine, open', filter: spec });
      expect(filterSpecSchema.safeParse(filter.filter).success).toBe(true);
      const tickets = await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'list_tickets', { projectId: w.projectId, filter: filter.filter });
      expect(tickets.isError, tickets.text).toBe(false);
      expect(tickets.text).toContain(w.ticketId);
      expect(tickets.text).not.toContain(w.secondTicketId); // CONTROL: the filter filtered
    });

    it('14.636 isolation: another person\'s saved filters in the same workspace never appear', async () => {
      const w = await buildDoorWorld(db, '2636');
      await saveFilters(w, w.owner, [{ id: 'o1', name: 'Owner only', filterSpec: { closed: false } }]);
      const member = await callTool(w.app, w.member.token, 'list_saved_filters', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId });
      expect(member.json).toEqual({ workspaceId: w.workspaceId, savedFilters: [] });
      const owner = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'list_saved_filters', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId });
      expect((owner.json as { savedFilters: Array<{ id: string }> }).savedFilters.map((f) => f.id)).toEqual(['o1']); // CONTROL
    });

    it('14.637 a guest gets their own filters; a stranger and an unknown workspace get the uniform sentence', async () => {
      const { app, otp } = buildOAuthApp(db);
      await app.ready();
      const owner = await signInWithPhone(app, otp, '+911100002637');
      const guest = await signInWithPhone(app, otp, '+911100002638');
      const stranger = await signInWithPhone(app, otp, '+911100002639');
      const ws = (await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: '/v1/workspaces', headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${owner.token}` }, payload: { name: 'Guest Co' } })).json<{ workspaceId: string }>().workspaceId;
      await grantRole(app, { userId: owner.userId, sessionToken: owner.token }, ws, guest.userId, 'guest');
      const saved = await app.inject({
        method: 'PUT', url: `/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=${ws}`, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${guest.token}` },
        payload: { savedFilters: [{ id: 'g1', name: 'Guest view', filterSpec: { closed: false } }], listColumns: {}, grouping: {} },
      });
      expect(saved.statusCode, saved.body).toBe(200);
      const as = async (userId: string, workspaceId: string) => callTool(app, await hostedToken(app, db.pool, userId), 'list_saved_filters', { workspaceId });
      const own = await as(guest.userId, ws);
      expect((own.json as { savedFilters: Array<{ id: string }> }).savedFilters.map((f) => f.id)).toEqual(['g1']); // a guest keeps their own
      for (const [label, userId, workspaceId] of [['stranger', stranger.userId, ws], ['unknown workspace', guest.userId, '01J0000000000000000000NONE']] as const) {
        const refused = await as(userId, workspaceId);
        expect(refused.isError, label).toBe(true);
        expect(refused.text, label).toBe('workspace not found or not visible to you');
      }
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.628 (hosted): (a) `putJson` → `postJson` → the route has no POST and the call fails (`done.isError`); (b) skip the PUT and answer `kept` → the app's own route reads `[]`.
  - 14.631: (a) answer `{ mutedKinds: kept, changed: true }` without the PUT → `list_notifications` still shows `mention`. The read-side filter itself is the route's; this case proves the tool's write reaches it.
  - 14.633 (hosted): none, guards existing behaviour (the route is keyed by the session user, `PUT /v1/me/notification-preferences`). Non-vacuous: a shared document would show the owner's `['assignment']` to the member.
  - 14.635: (a) keep the key `filterSpec` in the answer (no rename to `filter`) → the equality fails and `filter.filter` is `undefined`, so `list_tickets` returns both tickets; (b) answer `{ id, name }` only → same.
  - 14.636: none, guards existing behaviour (preferences are per person and workspace). Non-vacuous: the owner's own list is asserted `['o1']` in the same `it`.
  - 14.637 (hosted): (a) catch the 404 and answer `[]` → the stranger leg resolves.

  ```ts
  import { NOTIFICATION_KINDS, notificationPreferencesSchema, type NotificationKind } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  ```

  and change `import { postJson } from './submit.js';` to `import { postJson, putJson } from './submit.js';`. Below `MARK_ALL_PAGES`:

  ```ts
  /**
   * The app's own words for each kind (`packages/app-core/src/account/notifications-section.tsx:4-10`), copied. The record is
   * typed on `NotificationKind`, so a kind added to the domain is a type error here, and 14.632 reds if a description loses one.
   */
  const KIND_WORDS: Record<NotificationKind, string> = {
    assignment: 'A ticket is assigned to you',
    mention: 'You are mentioned in a comment, description or note',
    'contested-claim': 'A claim of yours is contested',
    'date-approaching': 'A date you are watching is approaching',
    'stale-ticket': 'A ticket has sat unmoved for a while',
  };
  const KINDS_IN_WORDS = NOTIFICATION_KINDS.map((kind) => `${kind} (${KIND_WORDS[kind]})`).join('; ');
  const kindSchema = z.enum(NOTIFICATION_KINDS);
  const muteShape = { mute: z.array(kindSchema).optional(), unmute: z.array(kindSchema).optional() };
  const PREFS_PATH = '/v1/me/notification-preferences';
  /** What `GET /v1/me/preferences` carries that this tool reads. `filterSpec` is passed through as stored: validating it again here would turn yesterday's saved view into an error. */
  const savedFiltersView = z.object({
    savedFilters: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), name: z.string(), filterSpec: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()) })),
  }).passthrough();

  /** The caller's muted kinds, strictly parsed: a malformed answer must never read as "nothing muted", because `set` would then write it back. */
  async function readMuted(session: Session): Promise<NotificationKind[]> {
    const parsed = notificationPreferencesSchema.safeParse(await session.get(PREFS_PATH));
    if (!parsed.success) throw new Error('your notification preferences could not be read, so nothing was changed');
    return parsed.data.mutedKinds;
  }
  ```

  Insert these three tools after the `mark_notifications_read` object:

  ```ts
      {
        name: 'get_notification_preferences',
        description:
          'Which kinds of notification you have muted. The setting is yours alone and covers every workspace. A muted kind is hidden from your inbox and its unread ' +
          `count (list_notifications omits it) but is still recorded, so unmuting brings it back. The kinds: ${KINDS_IN_WORDS}.`,
        inputSchema: {},
        handler: async () => {
          const mutedKinds = await readMuted(session);
          return { mutedKinds, kinds: NOTIFICATION_KINDS.map((kind) => ({ kind, muted: mutedKinds.includes(kind) })) };
        },
      },
      {
        name: 'set_notification_preferences',
        description:
          'Mute or unmute kinds of notification, changing only the kinds you name; every other kind stays as it is. mute: [...] hides those kinds from your inbox, ' +
          `unmute: [...] shows them again. Give at least one kind, and no kind in both lists. The kinds: ${KINDS_IN_WORDS}.`,
        inputSchema: muteShape,
        handler: async (args) => {
          const a = z.object(muteShape).parse(args);
          const mute = a.mute ?? [];
          const unmute = a.unmute ?? [];
          if (mute.length === 0 && unmute.length === 0) throw new Error('set_notification_preferences needs mute, unmute or both, with at least one kind');
          const both = mute.find((kind) => unmute.includes(kind));
          if (both !== undefined) throw new Error(`${both} is in both mute and unmute; choose one`);
          const current = await readMuted(session);
          const next = [...current];
          for (const kind of mute) if (!next.includes(kind)) next.push(kind);
          const kept = next.filter((kind) => !unmute.includes(kind));
          const changed = kept.length !== current.length || kept.some((kind, i) => kind !== current[i]);
          if (!changed) return { mutedKinds: current, changed: false };
          // The route replaces the whole document (`store.ts:37-55`), so the whole result is sent.
          const stored = notificationPreferencesSchema.parse(await putJson(session, PREFS_PATH, { mutedKinds: kept }));
          return { mutedKinds: stored.mutedKinds, changed: true };
        },
      },
      {
        name: 'list_saved_filters',
        description:
          'Your saved filters in one workspace (the named views on the project page): id, name and filter. Pass a filter to list_tickets or search exactly as it is. ' +
          'Only yours, and read-only.',
        inputSchema: { workspaceId: id },
        handler: async (args) => {
          const { workspaceId } = z.object({ workspaceId: id }).parse(args);
          const doc = await readOrNotFound(session, `/v1/me/preferences?${new URLSearchParams({ workspaceId }).toString()}`, 'workspace');
          const parsed = savedFiltersView.safeParse(doc);
          if (!parsed.success) throw new Error('your saved filters could not be read');
          return { workspaceId, savedFilters: parsed.data.savedFilters.map((f) => ({ id: f.id, name: f.name, filter: f.filterSpec })) };
        },
      },
  ```
