  `apps/server/test/mcp/helpers/email-world.ts` (copied from `buildDoorWorld`, `door-matrix.ts:37-75`, which cannot return the app's magic-link sender; this one can, so it can make verified email accounts):

  ```ts
  import { expect } from 'vitest';
  import { newUlid } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
  import type { LogMagicLinkSender } from '../../../src/auth/magic-link-sender.js';
  import type { LogOtpSender } from '../../../src/auth/otp-sender.js';
  import type { BuildTestAppOverrides } from '../../helpers/app.js';
  import type { TestDatabase } from '../../helpers/db.js';
  import { buildOAuthApp, hostedToken } from '../../helpers/oauth.js';
  import { signInWithEmail, signInWithPhone } from '../../helpers/session.js';
  import { event, grantRole, mustCommit, type Actor } from './sweep2.js';

  export type Person = Actor & { token: string; email?: string };
  export type EmailWorld = {
    app: FastifyInstance; db: TestDatabase; otp: LogOtpSender; magic: LogMagicLinkSender;
    owner: Person; member: Person; guest: Person; stranger: Person;
    workspaceId: string; workspaceName: string; projectId: string; projectName: string;
    ticketId: string; ticketKey: string; secondTicketId: string;
    /** A verified email account in no workspace, made the product's way (the magic link). */
    outsider(email: string): Promise<Person>;
  };

  /** An owner, a member (on the project), a guest, a stranger; one project, two tickets; and the means to make email accounts. `tail` is unique per test. */
  export async function buildEmailWorld(db: TestDatabase, tail: string, overrides: BuildTestAppOverrides = {}): Promise<EmailWorld> {
    const { app, otp, magic } = buildOAuthApp(db, overrides);
    await app.ready();
    const person = async (u: { userId: string; token: string }, email?: string): Promise<Person> =>
      ({ userId: u.userId, sessionToken: u.token, token: await hostedToken(app, db.pool, u.userId), ...(email === undefined ? {} : { email }) });
    const phone = (n: number) => `+9111000${tail}${n}`;
    const owner = await person(await signInWithPhone(app, otp, phone(1)));
    const member = await person(await signInWithPhone(app, otp, phone(2)));
    const guest = await person(await signInWithPhone(app, otp, phone(3)));
    const stranger = await person(await signInWithPhone(app, otp, phone(4)));
    const workspaceName = 'Email Co';
    const projectName = 'Counter';
    const found = await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: '/v1/workspaces', headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${owner.sessionToken}` }, payload: { name: workspaceName } });
    const workspaceId = (found.json() as { workspaceId: string }).workspaceId;
    const projectId = newUlid();
    const ticketId = newUlid();
    const secondTicketId = newUlid();
    const ev = (entity: { kind: 'project' | 'ticket' | 'membership'; id: string }, type: string, payload: unknown) => event(workspaceId, owner, entity, type, payload);
    await mustCommit(app, owner, [
      ev({ kind: 'project', id: projectId }, 'project.created', { name: projectName }),
      ev({ kind: 'project', id: projectId }, 'project.member_added', { userId: owner.userId, projectAdmin: true }),
      ev({ kind: 'membership', id: `${workspaceId}:${member.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: member.userId, role: 'member' }),
      ev({ kind: 'project', id: projectId }, 'project.member_added', { userId: member.userId, projectAdmin: false }),
      ev({ kind: 'ticket', id: ticketId }, 'ticket.created', { typeId: 'task', projectId, parentId: null, title: 'Fix login', start: 'backlog' }),
      ev({ kind: 'ticket', id: secondTicketId }, 'ticket.created', { typeId: 'task', projectId, parentId: null, title: 'Second ticket', start: 'backlog' }),
    ]);
    await grantRole(app, owner, workspaceId, guest.userId, 'guest');
    const ticketKey = (await app.inject({ method: 'GET', url: `/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/tickets/${ticketId}`, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${owner.sessionToken}` } })).json<{ key: string }>().key;
    expect(ticketKey).toMatch(/-\d+$/);
    return {
      app, db, otp, magic, owner, member, guest, stranger, workspaceId, workspaceName, projectId, projectName, ticketId, ticketKey, secondTicketId,
      outsider: async (email) => person(await signInWithEmail(app, magic, email), email),
    };
  }
  ```

  `packages/mcp-server/test/assignee-email.test.ts` (the package half: what the tool asks, shows and sends, with every request recorded). Rows are a TABLE so T8 adds `create_ticket` as a second row and every `for (const row of ROWS)` below then covers both:

  ```ts
  import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { HttpError } from '../src/http-client.js';
  import { writeTools } from '../src/tools/write-tools.js';
  import { eventBatches, recordingSession, type Recorded } from './fixtures/recording-session.js';

  const WS = 'W1';
  const T = 'T1';
  const P = 'P1';
  const ME = '01J0A5ER0000000000000000AA';
  const LOOKUP = `/v1/workspaces/${WS}/people-lookup`;
  const OUTSIDER = `/v1/workspaces/${WS}/assign-outsider`;
  const ASHA = { userId: '01J0ASHA0000000000000000AA', name: 'Asha Rao', email: 'asha@example.com', inWorkspace: true };
  const RAVI = { userId: '01J0RAVI0000000000000000AA', name: 'Ravi Menon', email: 'ravi@example.com', inWorkspace: false };
  type Person = typeof ASHA;
  const COMMITTED = { status: 'committed', eventId: 'e', seq: 1, serverTime: '2026-10-03T00:00:00.000Z' };
  const DETAIL = { key: 'CCSO-4', title: 'Fix login', ticketState: { fields: {}, workflowId: 'wf' }, rollup: null };
  const DIRECTORY = { workspaces: [{ workspaceId: WS, name: 'Corner Shop', prefix: 'CCSO', role: 'owner', archived: false, projects: [{ projectId: P, name: 'Counter', description: null, prefix: 'CC', archived: false }] }] };
  const miss = 'No user with that email. Someone you can already see in this workspace is assigned with assigneeId (find their userId with list_members).';
  const GUEST_SENTENCE = 'They will join this workspace as a guest and see only this ticket.';

  /** One row per tool that takes `assignee_email`. T8 appends `create_ticket`. */
  type Row = {
    tool: 'assign_ticket' | 'create_ticket';
    claim: 'ticket.assigned' | 'ticket.created';
    args(extra?: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown>;
    /** The first half of `wouldChange` for a person. */
    headline(p: Person): string;
    /** The GETs call 1 may make, in order, besides the lookup. */
    reads: string[];
    /** Whether call 1 reads a ticket (create has none yet). */
    readsTicket: boolean;
    /** The assignee the claim carries. */
    assigneeIn(claim: Record<string, unknown>): unknown;
  };
  export const ROWS: Row[] = [
    {
      tool: 'assign_ticket', claim: 'ticket.assigned', readsTicket: true, reads: [`/v1/workspaces/${WS}/tickets/${T}`],
      args: (extra = {}) => ({ workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T, assignee_email: ASHA.email, ...extra }),
      headline: (p) => `Assigns CCSO-4 "Fix login" to ${p.name} (${p.email}).`,
      assigneeIn: (claim) => (claim['payload'] as { assigneeId: unknown }).assigneeId,
    },
  ];

  type Stub = ReturnType<typeof recordingSession>;
  function stub(opts: { lookup?: (email: string, call: number) => Person | null; outsider?: (body: unknown) => unknown; events?: (events: Array<Record<string, unknown>>) => unknown } = {}): Stub & { lookups(): number } {
    let calls = 0;
    const byEmail = (email: string): Person | null => {
      const wanted = email.trim().toLowerCase();
      return wanted === ASHA.email ? ASHA : wanted === RAVI.email ? RAVI : null;
    };
    const rec = recordingSession({
      ...(opts.events === undefined ? {} : { events: opts.events }),
      routes: {
        [`GET /v1/workspaces/${WS}/tickets/${T}`]: DETAIL,
        'GET /v1/me/workspaces': DIRECTORY,
        [`POST ${LOOKUP}`]: (body) => {
          calls += 1;
          return { user: (opts.lookup ?? byEmail)((body as { email: string }).email, calls) };
        },
        [`POST ${OUTSIDER}`]: (body) => (opts.outsider === undefined ? { result: COMMITTED } : opts.outsider(body)),
      },
    });
    return { ...rec, lookups: () => calls };
  }
  const call = (row: Row, s: Stub, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => {
    const found = writeTools(s.session).find((t) => t.name === row.tool);
    if (found === undefined) throw new Error(`no ${row.tool}`);
    return found.handler(row.args(extra));
  };
  const at = (requests: Recorded[], path: string) => requests.filter((r) => r.path === path);
  /** Every request that is not a GET and not the lookup: a write of any kind. */
  const writes = (requests: Recorded[]) => requests.filter((r) => r.method !== 'GET' && r.path !== LOOKUP);
  type Preview = { preview: boolean; wouldChange: string; person: Person; joinsAsGuest: boolean; counts: object; nothingChanged: boolean; confirm: string };

  describe('assign_ticket: the arguments', () => {
    it('14.638 assigneeId alone is unchanged (a person, or null); both, neither, or confirm_assignee without an email is refused before any request', async () => {
      const row = ROWS[0]!;
      const alone = stub();
      const assign = writeTools(alone.session).find((t) => t.name === 'assign_ticket')!;
      await assign.handler({ workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T, assigneeId: 'U2' });
      await assign.handler({ workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T, assigneeId: null });
      const batches = eventBatches(alone.requests);
      expect(batches.map((b) => (b[0]!['payload'] as { assigneeId: unknown }).assigneeId)).toEqual(['U2', null]);
      expect(alone.requests.filter((r) => r.method === 'GET' || r.path === LOOKUP), 'the old path reads and looks up nothing').toEqual([]);
      for (const [label, args, sentence] of [
        ['both', { workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T, assigneeId: 'U2', assignee_email: ASHA.email }, 'exactly one of assigneeId'],
        ['neither', { workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T }, 'exactly one of assigneeId'],
        ['confirm without an email', { workspaceId: WS, ticketId: T, assigneeId: 'U2', confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId }, 'confirm_assignee only goes with assignee_email'],
      ] as const) {
        const s = stub();
        await expect(writeTools(s.session).find((t) => t.name === row.tool)!.handler({ ...args }), label).rejects.toThrow(sentence);
        expect(s.requests, `${label}: nothing sent`).toEqual([]);
      }
    });
  });

  describe('assignee_email: the confirmation (package)', () => {
    it('14.639 call 1 for a person in the workspace answers the preview with the app\'s sentence and makes only the reads and ONE lookup', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        const out = (await call(row, s)) as Preview;
        expect(out, row.tool).toMatchObject({ preview: true, person: ASHA, joinsAsGuest: false, counts: {}, nothingChanged: true, confirm: `confirm_assignee must be "${ASHA.userId}"` });
        expect(out.wouldChange).toBe(`${row.headline(ASHA)} Asha Rao is already in this workspace.`);
        expect(at(s.requests, LOOKUP)).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(s.requests.filter((r) => r.method === 'GET').map((r) => r.path)).toEqual(row.reads);
        expect(writes(s.requests), `${row.tool}: no event, no assign-outsider`).toEqual([]);
        expect(out.wouldChange).not.toMatch(/guest/i);
      }
    });

    it('14.640 call 1 for a person outside says in so many words they WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST, then the app\'s own sentence, and sends nothing', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        const out = (await call(row, s, { assignee_email: RAVI.email })) as Preview;
        expect(out, row.tool).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: true, person: RAVI, confirm: `confirm_assignee must be "${RAVI.userId}"` });
        expect(out.wouldChange).toBe(`${row.headline(RAVI)} Ravi Menon is not in this workspace and WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST. ${GUEST_SENTENCE}`);
        expect(writes(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('14.641 call 2 with the resolved userId for a person in the workspace sends ONE claim through /v1/events (origin mcp, the session user) and never calls assign-outsider', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        const out = (await call(row, s, { confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId })) as Record<string, unknown>;
        expect(out['status'] ?? (out['eventId'] === undefined ? undefined : 'committed'), row.tool).toBe('committed');
        const batches = eventBatches(s.requests);
        expect(batches).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(batches[0]).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(batches[0]![0]).toMatchObject({ type: row.claim, origin: 'mcp', actorId: ME, workspaceId: WS });
        expect(row.assigneeIn(batches[0]![0]!)).toBe(ASHA.userId);
        expect(at(s.requests, OUTSIDER)).toEqual([]);
        expect(at(s.requests, LOOKUP)).toHaveLength(1); // call 2 does its OWN lookup (this stub saw no call 1): a call 2 that trusted confirm_assignee without looking up would make 0
      }
    });

    it('14.642 call 2 for a person outside posts { userId, event } to assign-outsider once and sends nothing through /v1/events', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        const out = (await call(row, s, { assignee_email: RAVI.email, confirm_assignee: RAVI.userId })) as Record<string, unknown>;
        expect(out['status'] ?? (out['eventId'] === undefined ? undefined : 'committed'), row.tool).toBe('committed');
        const posts = at(s.requests, OUTSIDER);
        expect(posts).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(posts[0]!.body).toMatchObject({ userId: RAVI.userId, event: { type: row.claim, origin: 'mcp', actorId: ME, workspaceId: WS } });
        expect(row.assigneeIn((posts[0]!.body as { event: Record<string, unknown> }).event)).toBe(RAVI.userId);
        expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('14.643 a confirm_assignee that is not exactly the userId the email resolves to NOW (another id, the email, the name, a trailing space, the wrong case, empty) answers the fresh preview and writes nothing; the exact id writes', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        for (const wrong of [RAVI.userId, ASHA.email, ASHA.name, `${ASHA.userId} `, ASHA.userId.toLowerCase(), '']) {
          const out = (await call(row, s, { confirm_assignee: wrong })) as Preview;
          expect(out.preview, `${row.tool}: ${JSON.stringify(wrong)}`).toBe(true);
          expect(out.person.userId).toBe(ASHA.userId); // the person the email resolves to now is the one shown
        }
        expect(writes(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
        await call(row, s, { confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId }); // CONTROL, same `it`
        expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toHaveLength(1);
      }
    });

    it('14.644 re-resolution: when the email resolves to someone else between the calls, call 2 previews the NEW person and writes nothing; when it resolves to nobody, call 2 answers the miss', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const moved = stub({ lookup: (_email, n) => (n === 1 ? ASHA : RAVI) });
        const first = (await call(row, moved)) as Preview;
        expect(first.person.userId).toBe(ASHA.userId);
        const second = (await call(row, moved, { confirm_assignee: first.person.userId })) as Preview;
        expect(second).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: true, person: { userId: RAVI.userId } });
        expect(writes(moved.requests)).toEqual([]);
        const gone = stub({ lookup: (_email, n) => (n === 1 ? ASHA : null) });
        await call(row, gone);
        await expect(call(row, gone, { confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId }), row.tool).rejects.toThrow(miss);
        expect(writes(gone.requests)).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('14.645 no match answers "No user with that email." and the hint, on call 1 and on call 2, and writes nothing', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub({ lookup: () => null });
        await expect(call(row, s, { assignee_email: 'nobody@example.com' }), row.tool).rejects.toThrow(miss);
        await expect(call(row, s, { assignee_email: 'nobody@example.com', confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId })).rejects.toThrow(miss);
        expect(writes(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('14.646 a ticket the caller cannot see answers the ticket sentence and makes NO lookup; a lookup the server refuses (a guest, a stranger) answers the workspace sentence', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS.filter((r) => r.readsTicket)) {
        const s = stub({});
        const hidden = recordingSession({ routes: { [`POST ${LOOKUP}`]: { user: ASHA } } }); // no ticket route: a 404
        await expect(call(row, { ...s, ...hidden })).rejects.toThrow('ticket not found or not visible to you');
        expect(at(hidden.requests, LOOKUP), 'no lookup for a ticket the caller cannot see').toEqual([]);
      }
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const refused = recordingSession({
          routes: { [`GET /v1/workspaces/${WS}/tickets/${T}`]: DETAIL, 'GET /v1/me/workspaces': DIRECTORY, [`POST ${LOOKUP}`]: () => { throw new HttpError(404, { error: 'not_found' }); } },
        });
        await expect(call(row, { ...stub(), ...refused }), row.tool).rejects.toThrow('workspace not found or not visible to you');
        expect(writes(refused.requests)).toEqual([]);
      }
    });

    it('14.647 a refusal after confirmation is the server\'s words, never "answered 422": the outsider route\'s nested { result } is unwrapped, and the in-workspace refusal is spelled the same', async () => {
      const refusal = { status: 'refused', eventId: 'e', rule: 'permission.role', reason: 'That person is not in this workspace.' };
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const outsider = stub({ outsider: () => { throw new HttpError(422, { result: refusal }); } });
        const failure = await call(row, outsider, { assignee_email: RAVI.email, confirm_assignee: RAVI.userId }).catch((e: Error) => e);
        expect(failure).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
        expect((failure as Error).message, row.tool).toBe('refused by permission.role: That person is not in this workspace.');
        expect((failure as Error).message).not.toContain('answered 422');
        const inside = stub({ events: () => ({ results: [refusal] }) });
        await expect(call(row, inside, { confirm_assignee: ASHA.userId })).rejects.toThrow('refused by permission.role: That person is not in this workspace.');
      }
    });

    it('14.648 a 429 from the lookup answers a sentence saying lookups are rate limited and how long to wait, and writes nothing', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub({ lookup: () => { throw new HttpError(429, { error: 'rate_limited', retryAfterSeconds: 42 }); } });
        await expect(call(row, s), row.tool).rejects.toThrow('Looking people up by email is rate limited right now. Wait about 42 seconds and call this again; nothing was changed.');
        expect(writes(s.requests)).toEqual([]);
        await expect(call(row, stub())).resolves.toMatchObject({ preview: true }); // CONTROL: the same call without the 429
      }
    });

    it('14.649 the address goes to the lookup exactly as typed (the route trims and folds case), and the confirmation is the userId, so the case of the email never matters', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const s = stub();
        const typed = ' ASHA@Example.COM ';
        const first = (await call(row, s, { assignee_email: typed })) as Preview;
        expect(first.person.userId).toBe(ASHA.userId);
        expect(at(s.requests, LOOKUP)[0]!.body).toEqual({ email: typed });
        await call(row, s, { assignee_email: 'asha@example.com', confirm_assignee: first.person.userId });
        expect(eventBatches(s.requests)).toHaveLength(1);
      }
    });

    it('14.652 the preview says ADDED AS A GUEST if and only if the person is outside, and "guest" nowhere for a person in the workspace', async () => {
      for (const row of ROWS) {
        const inside = ((await call(row, stub())) as Preview).wouldChange;
        const outside = ((await call(row, stub(), { assignee_email: RAVI.email })) as Preview).wouldChange;
        expect(inside, row.tool).not.toContain('ADDED AS A GUEST');
        expect(inside).not.toMatch(/guest/i);
        expect(outside).toContain('ADDED AS A GUEST');
        expect(outside).toContain(GUEST_SENTENCE);
      }
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills (package; the rows loop, so T8's `create_ticket` row inherits each):
  - 14.638: (a) the exactly-one check becomes `a.assigneeId != null` (null counts as absent) → the `assigneeId: null` call throws "exactly one of"; (b) drop the exactly-one check and prefer `assigneeId` → the `both` row commits; (c) drop `confirm_assignee only goes with assignee_email` → that row commits; (d) read the ticket before the `assignee_email === undefined` branch → the old path's GET list is not empty.
  - 14.639: (a) submit the claim on call 1 → `writes` is not empty; (b) look up twice on call 1 → the lookup count is 2; (c) `guestSentence` ignores `inWorkspace` → `wouldChange` gains the guest sentence.
  - 14.640: (a) drop `willBeAddedAsGuest(...)` from `guestSentence` → the exact `wouldChange` fails; (b) `joinsAsGuest: person.inWorkspace` (inverted) → `joinsAsGuest` is false.
  - 14.641: (a) `submitForAssignee` always posts `assign-outsider` → the OUTSIDER list is not empty and no event batch is sent; (b) call 2 skips the lookup and trusts `confirm_assignee` as the userId → the lookup count is 0; (c) the claim's payload is `{ assigneeId: a.assignee_email }` → `assigneeIn` is the email.
  - 14.642: (a) `submitForAssignee` sends `submitOne` for everyone → no `assign-outsider` post, one event batch; (b) post `{ userId: person.email, event }` → the `userId` leg fails.
  - 14.643: (a) `confirmOrPreview` compares case-insensitively or trimmed (any edit in `resolveAssignee`'s call) → the wrong-case or trailing-space value writes; (b) compare `confirm` with `person.email` as well → the email value writes.
  - 14.644: (a) cache call 1's person in a module variable keyed by email → the moved case writes ASHA; (b) on a null lookup in call 2, fall back to `confirm_assignee` as the userId → the gone case writes.
  - 14.645: (a) answer a miss as a successful `{ found: false, message }` instead of throwing → the call resolves; (b) drop `NO_USER_HINT` from the thrown sentence → the exact sentence fails.
  - 14.646: (a) call 1 reads the ticket inside `headline` only (after the lookup) → the hidden ticket makes one lookup; (b) `lookUp` lets the 404 through → the text is `gisik-nokma answered 404`.
  - 14.647: (a) `submitForAssignee` returns the refused `result` without `resultError` → the call resolves and `failure` is not an `Error`; (b) `submitForAssignee` catches the error and rethrows `new Error(String(error))` → the message gains `Error: `.
  - 14.648: (a) `lookUp` does not call `rateLimitedError` → the text is `gisik-nokma answered 429`.
  - 14.649: (a) `lookUp` sends `{ email: email.trim().toLowerCase() }` → the recorded body differs from the typed address.
  - 14.652: (a) `alreadyInThisWorkspace` says `… is already in this workspace (as a guest or member).` → the member side matches `/guest/i`; (b) `guestSentence` drops `THEY_WILL_JOIN_AS_GUEST` → the outsider side fails.

  `apps/server/test/mcp/mcp-assignee-email-copy.test.ts` (14.651; no database):

  ```ts
  import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { OUTSIDER_JOINS_AS_GUEST, OUTSIDER_NOT_FOUND, alreadyInWorkspaceLine } from '@gisik-nokma/app-core';
  import { allTools, HttpError, type Session } from '@gisik-nokma/mcp-server';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T7, case 14.651. The MCP keeps its own copies of the app's two product sentences (the package must not depend
   * on app-core); this compares what the TOOL says with what the APP exports, so a rewording on either side fails here.
   * Needs app-core BUILT (`pnpm --filter @gisik-nokma/app-core build`).
   */
  const detail = { key: 'CCSO-4', title: 'Fix login', ticketState: { fields: {}, workflowId: 'wf' }, rollup: null };
  function sessionFor(user: { userId: string; name: string; email: string; inWorkspace: boolean } | null): Session {
    const unavailable = async () => { throw new Error('not stubbed'); };
    return {
      userId: () => '01J0A5ER0000000000000000AA',
      get: async (path) => { if (path.endsWith('/tickets/T1')) return detail; throw new HttpError(404, {}); },
      post: async (path) => { if (path.endsWith('/people-lookup')) return { user }; throw new HttpError(404, {}); },
      put: unavailable, postEvents: unavailable, getBytes: unavailable, postMultipart: unavailable,
    };
  }
  const assign = (session: Session, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
    allTools(session).find((t) => t.name === 'assign_ticket')!.handler({ workspaceId: 'W1', ticketId: 'T1', assignee_email: 'x@example.com', ...extra });

  describe('the sentences the assistant shows are the app\'s', () => {
    it('14.651 the guest sentence, the "already in this workspace" line and the miss are byte-identical to app-core\'s exports', async () => {
      expect(OUTSIDER_JOINS_AS_GUEST.length).toBeGreaterThan(10); // the comparison is not of two empty strings
      expect(OUTSIDER_NOT_FOUND.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
      const outside = (await assign(sessionFor({ userId: 'U9', name: 'Ravi Menon', email: 'ravi@example.com', inWorkspace: false }))) as { wouldChange: string };
      expect(outside.wouldChange.endsWith(OUTSIDER_JOINS_AS_GUEST)).toBe(true);
      const inside = (await assign(sessionFor({ userId: 'U8', name: 'Asha Rao', email: 'asha@example.com', inWorkspace: true }))) as { wouldChange: string };
      expect(inside.wouldChange.endsWith(alreadyInWorkspaceLine('Asha Rao'))).toBe(true);
      await expect(assign(sessionFor(null))).rejects.toThrow(new RegExp(`^${OUTSIDER_NOT_FOUND.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')}`));
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the check can fail — the tool's inside sentence is not the guest one.
      expect(inside.wouldChange.includes(OUTSIDER_JOINS_AS_GUEST)).toBe(false);
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills (14.651): (a) reword `THEY_WILL_JOIN_AS_GUEST` (e.g. `… and see this ticket only.`) → `endsWith(OUTSIDER_JOINS_AS_GUEST)` fails; (b) `alreadyInThisWorkspace` drops the full stop → `endsWith(alreadyInWorkspaceLine(...))` fails; (c) `NO_USER_WITH_THAT_EMAIL = 'No user has that email.'` → the anchored miss regex fails.

  `apps/server/test/mcp/mcp-assignee-email.test.ts` (the hosted half; the real routes, the real database). Rows again, so T8 appends `create_ticket`:

  ```ts
  import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from '../helpers/db.js';
  import { canSee } from '../../src/visibility/can-see.js';
  import { callTool } from './helpers/door-matrix.js';
  import { buildEmailWorld, type EmailWorld, type Person } from './helpers/email-world.js';
  import { event, mustCommit } from './helpers/sweep2.js';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T7, hosted half of cases 14.640-14.650 and 14.652 (the package half is `assignee-email.test.ts`).
   * Every call is a `tools/call` on `POST /mcp` against the real app and database. Run LOCALLY.
   */
  let db: TestDatabase;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDatabase();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db.drop();
  });

  type Reply = Record<string, unknown>;
  type Preview = { preview: boolean; wouldChange: string; person: { userId: string; name: string; inWorkspace: boolean }; joinsAsGuest: boolean; confirm: string };
  const MISS = 'No user with that email. Someone you can already see in this workspace is assigned with assigneeId (find their userId with list_members).';

  type HRow = {
    tool: 'assign_ticket' | 'create_ticket';
    claim: 'ticket.assigned' | 'ticket.created';
    args(w: EmailWorld, email: string, extra?: Reply): Reply;
    /** The ticket the committed reply is about. */
    ticketOf(w: EmailWorld, reply: Reply): string;
    /** Make the SERVER refuse the claim after call 1: returns the extra arguments call 2 must carry. */
    sabotage(w: EmailWorld): Promise<Reply>;
  };
  export const HROWS: HRow[] = [
    {
      tool: 'assign_ticket', claim: 'ticket.assigned',
      args: (w, email, extra = {}) => ({ workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: w.ticketId, assignee_email: email, ...extra }),
      ticketOf: (w) => w.ticketId,
      // The ticket is soft-deleted between the calls; call 2 does not read it, so the engine's refusal is what comes back.
      sabotage: async (w) => {
        await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'ticket', id: w.ticketId }, 'ticket.deleted', {})]);
        return {};
      },
    },
  ];

  const ask = (w: EmailWorld, who: Person, row: HRow, email: string, extra: Reply = {}) => callTool(w.app, who.token, row.tool, row.args(w, email, extra));
  const roleOf = async (w: EmailWorld, userId: string): Promise<string | null> =>
    (await w.db.pool.query<{ role: string }>(`SELECT role FROM proj_memberships WHERE workspace_id = $1 AND user_id = $2 AND scope_kind = 'workspace'`, [w.workspaceId, userId])).rows[0]?.role ?? null;
  const membershipLedger = async (w: EmailWorld, userId: string) =>
    (await w.db.pool.query<{ type: string; origin: string; actor_id: string }>(
      `SELECT type, origin, actor_id FROM events WHERE workspace_id = $1 AND entity_kind = 'membership' AND entity_id = $2 ORDER BY workspace_seq`, [w.workspaceId, `${w.workspaceId}:${userId}`])).rows;
  const claimsOn = async (w: EmailWorld, ticketId: string, type: string) =>
    (await w.db.pool.query<{ origin: string; actor_id: string; payload: { assigneeId?: string } }>(
      `SELECT origin, actor_id, payload FROM events WHERE entity_id = $1 AND type = $2 ORDER BY workspace_seq`, [ticketId, type])).rows;
  const assigneeOf = async (w: EmailWorld, ticketId: string) =>
    (await w.db.pool.query<{ assignee_id: string | null }>(`SELECT assignee_id FROM proj_tickets WHERE ticket_id = $1`, [ticketId])).rows[0]?.assignee_id ?? null;

  describe('assignee_email through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.640 call 1 for an outsider says they WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST and adds nobody: the roster has no new membership afterwards', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
        const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2640${i}`);
        const out = await w.outsider(`out-640-${row.tool}@example.test`);
        const preview = await ask(w, w.owner, row, out.email!);
        expect(preview.isError, preview.text).toBe(false);
        expect((preview.json as unknown as Preview)).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: true, person: { userId: out.userId, inWorkspace: false } });
        expect((preview.json as unknown as Preview).wouldChange).toContain('WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST');
        expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBeNull();
        expect(await membershipLedger(w, out.userId)).toEqual([]);
        // CONTROL, same `it`: a member of the workspace is previewed as already in.
        const inside = await w.outsider(`in-640-${row.tool}@example.test`);
        await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'membership', id: `${w.workspaceId}:${inside.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: inside.userId, role: 'member' })]);
        expect(((await ask(w, w.owner, row, inside.email!)).json as unknown as Preview)).toMatchObject({ joinsAsGuest: false, person: { inWorkspace: true } });
      }
    });

    it('14.641 call 2 for a person in the workspace commits ONE claim with origin mcp and the caller as actor, and adds no membership', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
        const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2641${i}`);
        const asha = await w.outsider(`asha-641-${row.tool}@example.test`);
        await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'membership', id: `${w.workspaceId}:${asha.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: asha.userId, role: 'member' })]);
        const preview = (await ask(w, w.owner, row, asha.email!)).json as unknown as Preview;
        const done = await ask(w, w.owner, row, asha.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId });
        expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
        const ticketId = row.ticketOf(w, done.json as Reply);
        const claims = await claimsOn(w, ticketId, row.claim);
        expect(claims.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ origin: 'mcp', actor_id: w.owner.userId });
        expect(claims.at(-1)!.payload.assigneeId).toBe(asha.userId);
        expect(await assigneeOf(w, ticketId)).toBe(asha.userId);
        expect((await membershipLedger(w, asha.userId)).map((m) => m.type)).toEqual(['membership.granted']); // only the setup's own grant
      }
    });

    it('14.642 call 2 for an outsider grants a GUEST membership (origin server, the caller as actor) and then commits the claim (origin mcp); the guest sees that ticket only', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
        const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2642${i}`);
        const out = await w.outsider(`out-642-${row.tool}@example.test`);
        const preview = (await ask(w, w.member, row, out.email!)).json as unknown as Preview; // a MEMBER may pull an outsider in (people.ts:154-156)
        const done = await ask(w, w.member, row, out.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId });
        expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
        const ticketId = row.ticketOf(w, done.json as Reply);
        expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBe('guest');
        expect(await membershipLedger(w, out.userId)).toEqual([{ type: 'membership.granted', origin: 'server', actor_id: w.member.userId }]);
        expect((await claimsOn(w, ticketId, row.claim)).at(-1)).toMatchObject({ origin: 'mcp', actor_id: w.member.userId });
        expect(await assigneeOf(w, ticketId)).toBe(out.userId);
        expect(await canSee(db.pool, out.userId, ticketId)).toBe(true);
        expect(await canSee(db.pool, out.userId, w.secondTicketId)).toBe(false); // CONTROL: only that ticket
      }
    });

    it('14.645 four different reasons for "no such person" are answered with the same sentence: an unknown address, an unverified account, a synthetic phone address, and an address held by two case-variant accounts', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2645${i}`);
      const unverified = await w.outsider(`unverified-645-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      await db.pool.query(`UPDATE "user" SET "emailVerified" = false WHERE id = $1`, [unverified.userId]);
      const phoneEmail = (await db.pool.query<{ email: string }>(`SELECT "email" FROM "user" WHERE id = $1`, [w.stranger.userId])).rows[0]!.email;
      await db.pool.query(`UPDATE "user" SET "emailVerified" = true WHERE id = $1`, [w.stranger.userId]); // verified on purpose: only the synthetic-address guard may answer null (6.207)
      await w.outsider(`twin-645-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      const second = await w.outsider(`twin2-645-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      await db.pool.query(`UPDATE "user" SET "email" = $2 WHERE id = $1`, [second.userId, `Twin-645-${row.tool}@Example.test`]);
      for (const [label, email] of [['unknown', `nobody-645-${row.tool}@example.test`], ['unverified', unverified.email!], ['synthetic', phoneEmail], ['case twins', `twin-645-${row.tool}@example.test`]] as const) {
        const miss = await ask(w, w.owner, row, email);
        expect(miss.isError, label).toBe(true);
        expect(miss.text, label).toBe(MISS);
      }
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a verified, unique address resolves.
      const real = await w.outsider(`real-645-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      expect(((await ask(w, w.owner, row, real.email!)).json as unknown as Preview).person.userId).toBe(real.userId);
      }
    });

    it('14.646 a guest caller gets the workspace sentence from the lookup; a stranger is stopped one step earlier by the ticket read; a member is not refused', async () => {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '2646');
      const row = HROWS[0]!;
      const target = await w.outsider('target-646@example.test');
      // The guest must be able to READ the ticket to reach the lookup at all: assign it to them first.
      await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'ticket', id: w.ticketId }, 'ticket.assigned', { assigneeId: w.guest.userId })]);
      const guest = await ask(w, w.guest, row, target.email!);
      expect(guest.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(guest.text).toBe('workspace not found or not visible to you');
      const stranger = await ask(w, w.stranger, row, target.email!);
      expect(stranger.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(stranger.text).toBe('ticket not found or not visible to you');
      expect((await ask(w, w.member, row, target.email!)).isError).toBe(false); // CONTROL
    });

    it('14.647 a refusal after confirmation is the server\'s words (never "answered 422"); after a refused outsider assignment no guest membership remains', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
        for (const outsider of [false, true]) {
          const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2647${i}${outsider ? 1 : 0}`);
          const who = await w.outsider(`who-647-${row.tool}-${outsider}@example.test`);
          if (!outsider) await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'membership', id: `${w.workspaceId}:${who.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: who.userId, role: 'member' })]);
          const preview = (await ask(w, w.member, row, who.email!)).json as unknown as Preview;
          // Precondition: call 1 resolved THIS person on the arm under test, so call 2 below is a confirmed write, not a fresh preview.
          expect(preview).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: outsider, person: { userId: who.userId } });
          const extra = await row.sabotage(w);
          const refused = await ask(w, w.member, row, who.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId, ...extra });
          expect(refused.isError, `${row.tool} outsider=${outsider}`).toBe(true);
          expect(refused.text).toMatch(/^refused by [\w.-]+: .+/);
          expect(refused.text).not.toContain('answered 422');
          if (outsider) {
            expect(await roleOf(w, who.userId)).toBeNull();
            expect((await membershipLedger(w, who.userId)).map((m) => m.type)).toEqual(['membership.granted', 'membership.revoked']); // the compensation
          }
          // CONTROL, same `it`: the same confirmation against a target the sabotage did not touch commits (assign: the second
          // ticket; create: the ordinary arguments, without the sabotage's extra).
          const untouched = row.tool === 'assign_ticket' ? { ticketId: w.secondTicketId } : {};
          const control = await ask(w, w.member, row, who.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId, ...untouched });
          expect(control.isError, `${row.tool} outsider=${outsider} control: ${control.text}`).toBe(false);
        }
      }
    });

    it('14.648 when the lookup budget is spent the assistant is told in a sentence, not "answered 429"', async () => {
      const security = { webOrigin: null, trustProxy: false, rateLimit: { authMax: 1000, eventsMax: 1000, inviteMax: 1, mcpMax: 1000, signInMax: 1000, windowSec: 60 } };
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '2648', { security });
      const row = HROWS[0]!;
      const target = await w.outsider('target-648@example.test');
      expect((await ask(w, w.owner, row, target.email!)).isError).toBe(false); // the one lookup the budget allows
      const second = await ask(w, w.owner, row, target.email!);
      expect(second.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(second.text).toMatch(/^Looking people up by email is rate limited right now\. Wait about \d+ seconds? and call this again; nothing was changed\.$/);
    });

    it('14.649 the address is trimmed and case-folded by the route: " ASHA@Example.TEST " resolves to the same person, and the confirmation (a userId) is unaffected', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2649${i}`);
      const asha = await w.outsider(`asha-649-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      const padded = ((await ask(w, w.owner, row, ` ASHA-649-${row.tool}@Example.TEST `)).json as unknown as Preview);
      expect(padded.person.userId).toBe(asha.userId);
      const done = await ask(w, w.owner, row, `asha-649-${row.tool}@example.test`, { confirm_assignee: padded.person.userId });
      expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
      }
    });

    it('14.650 the assignment is in the ticket\'s History as the assistant\'s, assign_ticket with assigneeId null removes it, and the guest membership stays', async () => {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '2650');
      const row = HROWS[0]!;
      const out = await w.outsider('out-650@example.test');
      const preview = (await ask(w, w.owner, row, out.email!)).json as unknown as Preview;
      const assigned = await ask(w, w.owner, row, out.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId });
      expect(assigned.isError, assigned.text).toBe(false);
      const history = (await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'get_ticket_history', { ticketId: w.ticketId })).json as { events: Array<{ type: string; origin: string }> };
      expect(history.events.find((e) => e.type === 'ticket.assigned')).toMatchObject({ origin: 'mcp' });
      expect(await assigneeOf(w, w.ticketId)).toBe(out.userId); // precondition: the unassign below has something to remove
      expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBe('guest'); // precondition: the grant the unassign must leave alone
      const removed = await callTool(w.app, w.owner.token, 'assign_ticket', { workspaceId: w.workspaceId, ticketId: w.ticketId, assigneeId: null });
      expect(removed.isError, removed.text).toBe(false);
      expect(await assigneeOf(w, w.ticketId)).toBeNull();
      expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBe('guest'); // unassigning does not undo the grant (as in the app)
    });

    it('14.652 a person who is already a guest is shown as already in the workspace, and assigning them grants nothing a second time', async () => {
      for (const [i, row] of HROWS.entries()) {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, `2652${i}`);
      const guest = await w.outsider(`guest-652-${row.tool}@example.test`);
      await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'membership', id: `${w.workspaceId}:${guest.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: guest.userId, role: 'guest' })]);
      const preview = (await ask(w, w.owner, row, guest.email!)).json as unknown as Preview;
      expect(preview).toMatchObject({ joinsAsGuest: false, person: { inWorkspace: true } });
      expect(preview.wouldChange).not.toContain('ADDED AS A GUEST');
      const done = await ask(w, w.owner, row, guest.email!, { confirm_assignee: preview.person.userId });
      expect(done.isError, done.text).toBe(false);
      expect((await membershipLedger(w, guest.userId)).map((m) => m.type)).toEqual(['membership.granted']); // the setup's own grant, no second one, no demotion
      expect(await roleOf(w, guest.userId)).toBe('guest');
      }
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills (hosted; the rows loop, so T8's row inherits each):
  - 14.640: (a) call 1 posts `assign-outsider` → the outsider has a `guest` role and a membership event.
  - 14.641: (a) call 2 compares `confirm_assignee` with something other than the userId (say `person.email`) and so always previews → `done` is a preview, no claim is in the ledger and `claims.at(-1)` is undefined; (b) the claim carries `{ assigneeId: a.assignee_email }` → `payload.assigneeId` and `assigneeOf` differ. (`submitForAssignee` always posting `assign-outsider` would NOT fail here: for a member the route grants nothing and commits the same claim. The package 14.641 owns that mutant.)
  - 14.642: (a) send the outsider's claim through `/v1/events` → `refused by permission.role: …` (not in the workspace), `done.isError` is true.
  - 14.645: (a) `resolveAssignee` answers a different sentence when the lookup is `null` → all four rows fail. The four reasons themselves (unknown, unverified, synthetic, case twins) are the route's `lookupPerson` rules, guarded here as existing behaviour.
  - 14.646: (a) call 1 looks the address up before reading the ticket → the stranger gets `workspace not found…` (the lookup's 404), not the ticket sentence.
  - 14.647: (a) `submitForAssignee` returns a refused result as success → `refused.isError` is false; (b) `postOutsider` rethrows the 422 (T2 code) → `answered 422` appears.
  - 14.648: (a) `lookUp` lets the 429 through → the text is `gisik-nokma answered 429`.
  - 14.649: none of this task's code folds case (the route does); it pins that the tool does NOT reject or alter the padded address. (a) A tool-side email validation (`z.string().email()` on `assignee_email`) → the padded address is refused.
  - 14.650: (a) the exactly-one check treats `assigneeId: null` as absent (`!= null`) → the unassign throws.
  - 14.652: none beyond the package 14.652 (whose mutants are the guest wording). The hosted half guards existing behaviour: `lookupPerson` answers `inWorkspace: true` for a guest, and `assign-outsider` never re-grants or demotes (`people.test.ts` 6.214). It is non-vacuous because a second grant or a demotion appends a membership event and the ledger leg fails.

  Every hosted loop builds one world per row with a tail that ends in the row's index (`2640${i}`): the database is shared by the whole file and `signInWithPhone` signs the SAME person in again for a repeated number, so T8's second row must not reuse a tail, and every email address carries `${row.tool}` for the same reason.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

  `packages/mcp-server/src/tools/assignee-email.ts`:

  ```ts
  import { z } from 'zod';
  import type { SubmittedEvent } from '@gisik-nokma/domain';
  import { HttpError } from '../http-client.js';
  import type { Session } from '../session.js';
  import { confirmOrPreview, type Preview } from './preview.js';
  import { postJson, postOutsider, rateLimitedError, resultError, submitOne } from './submit.js';

  /**
   * Assigning by email address (MCP sweep 2 §7): the app's own flow (find, show, assign) in two stateless calls. Call 1 looks the
   * address up and answers a preview that names the person; call 2 repeats the call with `confirm_assignee` set to that person's
   * userId, looks the address up AGAIN (nothing is remembered between calls, `directory.ts:10-12`) and writes only if the same
   * userId still comes back. The userId, not the email, is the confirmation: the caller already holds the email, so echoing it proves
   * nothing, while the id exists only in the preview.
   */
  export type LookedUpPerson = { userId: string; name: string; email: string; inWorkspace: boolean };
  const personSchema = z.object({ userId: z.string().min(1), name: z.string(), email: z.string(), inWorkspace: z.boolean() });

  /** The app's two product sentences, COPIED: `packages/app-core/src/people/copy.ts:11-12` and `:18-20`. 14.651 compares them with the app's own exports. */
  export const NO_USER_WITH_THAT_EMAIL = 'No user with that email.';
  export const THEY_WILL_JOIN_AS_GUEST = 'They will join this workspace as a guest and see only this ticket.';
  export const alreadyInThisWorkspace = (name: string): string => `${name} is already in this workspace.`;
  /** Owner ruling 11c: said in so many words, and the capitals are in the text so the effect cannot be read past. */
  export const willBeAddedAsGuest = (name: string): string => `${name} is not in this workspace and WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST.`;
  export const NO_USER_HINT = 'Someone you can already see in this workspace is assigned with assigneeId (find their userId with list_members).';
  const WORKSPACE_GONE = 'workspace not found or not visible to you';

  /** The two arguments `assign_ticket` and `create_ticket` share (S2-9: `assignee_email` as the owner wrote it; `confirm_assignee` follows `confirm_name`). */
  export const EMAIL_FIELDS = { assignee_email: z.string().min(1).optional(), confirm_assignee: z.string().optional() };

  export function guestSentence(person: LookedUpPerson): string {
    return person.inWorkspace ? alreadyInThisWorkspace(person.name) : `${willBeAddedAsGuest(person.name)} ${THEY_WILL_JOIN_AS_GUEST}`;
  }

  /** `POST …/people-lookup { email }` (`routes/people.ts:112-135`): the one verified account with exactly that address, or `null`. */
  async function lookUp(session: Session, workspaceId: string, email: string): Promise<LookedUpPerson | null> {
    try {
      const body = (await postJson(session, `/v1/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}/people-lookup`, { email })) as { user?: unknown } | null;
      return body?.user == null ? null : personSchema.parse(body.user);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError) {
        const limited = rateLimitedError(error, 'Looking people up by email');
        if (limited !== null) throw limited;
        if (error.status === 404) throw new Error(WORKSPACE_GONE); // a guest, a non-member and a missing workspace are told the same thing
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  export type Resolved = { preview: Preview & { person: LookedUpPerson; joinsAsGuest: boolean } } | { person: LookedUpPerson };

  /**
   * Look the address up and either answer the preview of whoever it resolves to NOW, or (when `confirm` is exactly that person's userId)
   * hand the person back to be acted on. `headline` is the tool's own first sentence, built only if a preview is needed.
   */
  export async function resolveAssignee(
    session: Session,
    workspaceId: string,
    email: string,
    confirm: string | undefined,
    headline: (person: LookedUpPerson) => Promise<string>,
  ): Promise<Resolved> {
    const person = await lookUp(session, workspaceId, email);
    if (person === null) throw new Error(`${NO_USER_WITH_THAT_EMAIL} ${NO_USER_HINT}`);
    const preview = await confirmOrPreview(person.userId, confirm, async () => ({ sentence: `${await headline(person)} ${guestSentence(person)}` }), 'confirm_assignee');
    if (preview !== null) return { preview: { ...preview, person, joinsAsGuest: !person.inWorkspace } };
    return { person };
  }

  /**
   * The claim, through the door that fits the person: an ordinary event for someone in the workspace, `assign-outsider` for someone
   * outside (it grants a guest membership, submits the caller's own claim, and takes the grant back if the claim is refused,
   * `routes/people.ts:137-221`). A refusal is the server's words either way.
   */
  export async function submitForAssignee(session: Session, workspaceId: string, person: LookedUpPerson, event: SubmittedEvent): Promise<unknown> {
    if (person.inWorkspace) return submitOne(session, event);
    try {
      const result = await postOutsider(session, workspaceId, person.userId, event);
      const failure = resultError(result);
      if (failure !== null) throw failure;
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError && error.status === 404) throw new Error(WORKSPACE_GONE);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  ```

      {
        name: 'assign_ticket',
        description:
          'Assign a ticket to somebody, or pass assigneeId null to leave it unassigned. To assign someone by their email address instead, pass assignee_email (and no assigneeId): ' +
          'the first call looks the address up and answers a PREVIEW naming the person and saying whether they are already in the workspace or WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST (who would then see only this ticket); ' +
          'nothing changes. Repeat the call with confirm_assignee set to that person\'s userId from the preview to assign. The address must belong to a person with a verified account.',
        inputSchema: {
          workspaceId: z.string().min(1),
          ticketId: z.string().min(1),
          assigneeId: z.string().min(1).nullable().optional(),
          ...EMAIL_FIELDS,
        },
        handler: async (args) => {
          const a = z
            .object({ workspaceId: z.string().min(1), ticketId: z.string().min(1), assigneeId: z.string().min(1).nullable().optional(), ...EMAIL_FIELDS })
            .parse(args);
          if ((a.assigneeId !== undefined) === (a.assignee_email !== undefined)) {
            throw new Error('assign_ticket takes exactly one of assigneeId (a person\'s id, or null to unassign) and assignee_email (an address, confirmed in a second call)');
          }
          if (a.confirm_assignee !== undefined && a.assignee_email === undefined) throw new Error('confirm_assignee only goes with assignee_email');
          if (a.assignee_email === undefined) {
            return submitOne(session, envelope(session, a.workspaceId, a.ticketId, 'ticket.assigned', { assigneeId: a.assigneeId ?? null }));
          }
          // Call 1 reads the ticket FIRST, so a ticket the caller cannot see is answered before any lookup (AE-11). Call 2 reads it only to
          // name it in a fresh preview: the server's own refusal then covers a ticket that went away between the calls.
          let ticket = a.confirm_assignee === undefined ? await readTicket(session, a.workspaceId, a.ticketId) : undefined;
          const resolved = await resolveAssignee(session, a.workspaceId, a.assignee_email, a.confirm_assignee, async (person) => {
            ticket ??= await readTicket(session, a.workspaceId, a.ticketId);
            return `Assigns ${ticket.key ?? a.ticketId} "${ticket.title}" to ${person.name} (${person.email}).`;
          });
          if ('preview' in resolved) return resolved.preview;
          return submitForAssignee(session, a.workspaceId, resolved.person, envelope(session, a.workspaceId, a.ticketId, 'ticket.assigned', { assigneeId: resolved.person.userId }));
        },
      },

  ```ts
  describe('create_ticket by email through the hosted door', () => {
    const CREATE = (): HRow => HROWS.find((r) => r.tool === 'create_ticket')!;
    const ticketCount = async (w: EmailWorld) => Number((await w.db.pool.query(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM proj_tickets WHERE project_id = $1`, [w.projectId])).rows[0].n);
    const eventTotal = async (w: EmailWorld) => Number((await w.db.pool.query(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM events WHERE workspace_id = $1`, [w.workspaceId])).rows[0].n);

    it('14.654 call 1 for an outsider creates no ticket and appends no event; the project\'s tickets and the ledger are unchanged', async () => {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '26540');
      const out = await w.outsider('out-654@example.test');
      const before = { tickets: await ticketCount(w), events: await eventTotal(w) };
      const preview = await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!);
      expect((preview.json as unknown as Preview)).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: true });
      expect({ tickets: await ticketCount(w), events: await eventTotal(w) }).toEqual(before);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: call 2 creates exactly one ticket.
      await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!, { confirm_assignee: out.userId });
      expect(await ticketCount(w)).toBe(before.tickets + 1);
    });

    it('14.655 the created ticket is assigned to the resolved person from birth, in both arms, and the guest sees it', async () => {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '26550');
      const inside = await w.outsider('inside-655@example.test');
      await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'membership', id: `${w.workspaceId}:${inside.userId}` }, 'membership.granted', { userId: inside.userId, role: 'member' })]);
      const a = await ask(w, w.owner, CREATE(), inside.email!, { confirm_assignee: inside.userId });
      expect(a.isError, a.text).toBe(false);
      const aId = (a.json as { ticketId: string }).ticketId;
      expect(await assigneeOf(w, aId)).toBe(inside.userId);
      const out = await w.outsider('out-655@example.test');
      const b = await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!, { confirm_assignee: out.userId });
      expect(b.isError, b.text).toBe(false);
      const bId = (b.json as { ticketId: string }).ticketId;
      expect(await assigneeOf(w, bId)).toBe(out.userId);
      expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBe('guest');
      expect(await canSee(db.pool, out.userId, bId)).toBe(true);
      expect(await canSee(db.pool, out.userId, aId)).toBe(false); // CONTROL: only the ticket they were born onto
    });

    it('14.656 a refusal after confirmation (an unknown ticket type; a project the caller is not in) is the server\'s words, creates nothing, and leaves no guest membership; the ledger shows the grant taken back', async () => {
      const w = await buildEmailWorld(db, '26560');
      const out = await w.outsider('out-656@example.test');
      const before = await ticketCount(w);
      const badType = await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!, { confirm_assignee: out.userId, typeId: 'no-such-type' });
      expect(badType.isError).toBe(true);
      expect(badType.text).toMatch(/^refused by [\w.-]+: .+/);
      expect(badType.text).not.toContain('answered 422');
      // A second project the member is NOT on.
      const other = newUlid();
      await mustCommit(w.app, w.owner, [
        event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'project', id: other }, 'project.created', { name: 'Closed room' }),
        event(w.workspaceId, w.owner, { kind: 'project', id: other }, 'project.member_added', { userId: w.owner.userId, projectAdmin: true }),
      ]);
      const notOn = await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!, { confirm_assignee: out.userId, projectId: other });
      expect(notOn.isError, 'if the server lets a workspace member create in a project they are not on, say so in the HANDOFF and drop this arm: the unknown-type arm above already owns the claim').toBe(true);
      expect(await ticketCount(w)).toBe(before);
      expect(await roleOf(w, out.userId)).toBeNull();
      const ledger = (await membershipLedger(w, out.userId)).map((m) => m.type);
      expect(ledger.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2); // the unknown-type arm reaches the engine AFTER the grant
      expect(ledger.filter((t) => t === 'membership.granted')).toHaveLength(ledger.filter((t) => t === 'membership.revoked').length); // every grant was taken back
      expect(ledger.at(-1)).toBe('membership.revoked');
      // CONTROL, same `it`: the same confirmation with the ordinary arguments creates.
      const ok = await ask(w, w.member, CREATE(), out.email!, { confirm_assignee: out.userId });
      expect(ok.isError, ok.text).toBe(false);
      expect(await ticketCount(w)).toBe(before + 1);
    });
  });
  ```

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T9, case 14.658. Manual §16 names each new ability in plain words and tells the reader that assigning by email asks first and
   * that a person outside the workspace is ADDED AS A GUEST; the Help search finds it. Against the REAL generated content, like
   * `connecting-assistants.test.ts`, so a stale `pnpm help:generate` reddens here as well as in `pnpm check:help`.
   */
  const SECTION_ID = '16-connecting-claude-or-chatgpt';
  const section = () => {
    const manual = HELP_CONTENT.find((d) => d.id === 'manual');
    const found = manual?.sections.find((s) => s.id === SECTION_ID);
    if (found === undefined) throw new Error(`no §16 (${SECTION_ID})`);
    return found;
  };
  const can = (): string => {
    const e: HelpEntry | undefined = section().entries.find((x) => x.title === 'What your assistant can do');
    if (e === undefined) throw new Error('§16 has no "What your assistant can do"');
    return entryText(e).toLowerCase();
  };

        'by email address', 'asks you first', 'added as a guest', 'only that ticket',
      ]) expect(text, phrase).toContain(phrase);
      const found = (q: string) => {
        const r = searchHelp(HELP_CONTENT, q, 50);
        return r.kind === 'matches' && r.matches.some((m) => m.document === 'manual' && m.sectionId === SECTION_ID);
      };
      expect(found('saved filters')).toBe(true);
      expect(found('added as a guest')).toBe(true);
      // CONTROL, same `it`: a phrase the section does not carry is not found there, and the old words are still present.
      expect(found('passkey')).toBe(false);
      expect(text).toContain('exact name');
    });
  });

  `apps/server/test/mcp/mcp-sweep-2-round-trip.test.ts` (14.659; sibling of `mcp-everything-round-trip.test.ts`, copy its `rpc`/`raw`/`ok` helpers verbatim from `:36-60`):

  ```ts
  import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
  import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
  import { buildOAuthApp, hostedToken } from '../helpers/oauth.js';
  import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from '../helpers/db.js';
  import { signInWithEmail, signInWithPhone } from '../helpers/session.js';

  /**
   * MCP sweep 2 T9, case 14.659. One person, one HOSTED token, every step a `tools/call` against `POST /mcp` on the real app and a real
   * database, nothing stubbed: the notes on a ticket, a restored note version, close three (one cannot close), mute and unmute, a saved
   * filter used in `list_tickets`, and assign-by-email plus create-by-email for an outsider, each with its preview first. Run LOCALLY.
   */
  let db: TestDatabase;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    db = await createTestDatabase();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await db.drop();
  });

  type Content = Array<{ type: string; text?: string }>;

  describe('MCP sweep 2, end to end through the hosted door', () => {
    it('14.659 notes on a ticket → restore a version → close three → mute/unmute → saved filter → assign and create by email (preview, then confirm)', async () => {
      const { app, otp, magic } = buildOAuthApp(db);
      await app.ready();
      const owner = await signInWithPhone(app, otp, '+911100002659');
      const token = await hostedToken(app, db.pool, owner.userId);
      const outsider = await signInWithEmail(app, magic, 'sweep2-out@example.test');
      const second = await signInWithEmail(app, magic, 'sweep2-second@example.test');

      const rpc = (method: string, params: unknown) =>
        (app as FastifyInstance).inject({
          method: 'POST', url: '/mcp',
          headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', accept: 'application/json, text/event-stream', 'mcp-protocol-version': '2025-06-18', authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
          payload: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, method, params },
        });
      const raw = async (name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        const res = await rpc('tools/call', { name, arguments: args });
        expect(res.statusCode, res.body.slice(0, 200)).toBe(200);
        const result = res.json().result as { isError?: boolean; content: Content };
        const text = result.content.find((c) => c.type === 'text')?.text ?? '';
        return { isError: result.isError === true, text, json: result.isError === true ? null : (JSON.parse(text) as Record<string, unknown>) };
      };
      const ok = async (name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        const r = await raw(name, args);
        expect(r.isError, `${name}: ${r.text}`).toBe(false);
        return r.json as Record<string, unknown>;
      };

      const names = ((await rpc('tools/list', {})).json().result as { tools: Array<{ name: string }> }).tools.map((t) => t.name);
      for (const used of ['restore_note_version', 'close_tickets', 'get_notification_preferences', 'set_notification_preferences', 'list_saved_filters']) expect(names, used).toContain(used);

      const ws = (await ok('create_workspace', { name: 'Sweep Two Co' }))['workspaceId'] as string;
      const projectId = (await ok('create_project', { workspaceId: ws, name: 'Counter' }))['projectId'] as string;
      const ticket = async (title: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
        (await ok('create_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, projectId, typeId: 'task', title, start: 'focus', ...extra }))['ticketId'] as string;

      // 1. A note attached to a ticket shows on get_ticket.
      const t1 = await ticket('Chiller');
      const note = (await ok('create_note', { workspaceId: ws, title: 'Cold room', scope: 'workspace', markdown: 'first draft' }))['noteId'] as string;
      await ok('link_note', { noteId: note, ticketId: t1, action: 'attach' });
      const shown = (await ok('get_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: t1 })) as { notes: Array<{ noteId: string; title: string }>; notesTotal: number };
      expect(shown.notes.map((n) => n.noteId)).toEqual([note]);
      expect(shown.notesTotal).toBe(1);
      const bare = await ticket('No notes here');
      expect(((await ok('get_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: bare })) as { notes: unknown[]; notesTotal: number })).toMatchObject({ notes: [], notesTotal: 0 }); // CONTROL

      // 2. Restore an earlier version of the note.
      await ok('update_note', { noteId: note, markdown: 'second draft' });
      const versions = ((await ok('get_note_history', { noteId: note })) as { versions: Array<{ eventId: string }> }).versions;
      expect(versions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
      let restoredFrom: string | undefined;
      for (const v of versions) {
        // The born-empty first version has no body (the route answers 404), so a read can be an error: look at the answer, do not demand it.
        const read = await raw('get_note_history', { noteId: note, versionId: v.eventId });
        if (!read.isError && String(read.json?.['markdown'] ?? '').includes('first draft')) restoredFrom = v.eventId;
      }
      expect(restoredFrom, 'one saved version holds the first draft').toBeDefined();
      const back = await ok('restore_note_version', { noteId: note, versionId: restoredFrom });
      expect(back).toMatchObject({ noteId: note, restored: true });
      expect((await ok('get_note', { noteId: note }))['markdown']).toContain('first draft');

      // 3. Close three: two are done, one is not. Preview first, then the count.
      const a = await ticket('Close A');
      const b = await ticket('Close B');
      const c = await ticket('Close C (still open)');
      for (const id of [a, b]) {
        await ok('transition_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: id, to: 'doing' });
        await ok('transition_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: id, to: 'done' });
      }
      const closedEvents = async () => (await db.pool.query(`SELECT 1 FROM events WHERE workspace_id = $1 AND type = 'ticket.closed'`, [ws])).rowCount;
      const before = await closedEvents();
      const previewed = await ok('close_tickets', { workspaceId: ws, ticketIds: [a, b, c] });
      expect(previewed).toMatchObject({ preview: true, nothingChanged: true });
      expect(await closedEvents()).toBe(before);
      const closed = (await ok('close_tickets', { workspaceId: ws, ticketIds: [a, b, c], confirm_count: '3' })) as { closed: number; refused: number; results: Array<{ ticketId: string; status: string }> };
      expect(closed).toMatchObject({ closed: 2, refused: 1 });
      expect(closed.results.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['closed', 'closed', 'refused']);
      expect(await closedEvents()).toBe((before ?? 0) + 2); // CONTROL: exactly the two that could close

      // 4. Mute and unmute; the stored preference is what the app reads.
      expect(await ok('set_notification_preferences', { mute: ['mention'] })).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['mention'], changed: true });
      expect(await ok('get_notification_preferences', {})).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: ['mention'] });
      expect(await ok('set_notification_preferences', { unmute: ['mention'] })).toMatchObject({ mutedKinds: [], changed: true });
      expect(await ok('set_notification_preferences', { unmute: ['mention'] })).toMatchObject({ changed: false }); // CONTROL: a no-op says so (14.629 pins that it sends no PUT)

      // 5. A saved filter, used as list_tickets' filter.
      const spec = { closed: false };
      const saved = await (app as FastifyInstance).inject({
        method: 'PUT', url: `/v1/me/preferences?workspaceId=${ws}`, headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${owner.token}` },
        payload: { savedFilters: [{ id: 'f1', name: 'Still open', filterSpec: spec }], listColumns: {}, grouping: {} },
      });
      expect(saved.statusCode, saved.body).toBe(200);
      const filters = (await ok('list_saved_filters', { workspaceId: ws })) as { savedFilters: Array<{ id: string; filter: unknown }> };
      expect(filters.savedFilters).toEqual([{ id: 'f1', name: 'Still open', filter: spec }]);
      // Precondition: unfiltered, the closed ticket IS listed (a FilterSpec without `closed` is "all", `filter-spec.ts:151-152`),
      // so the control below shows the filter working rather than a default that already hides closed tickets.
      expect(JSON.stringify(await ok('list_tickets', { projectId }))).toContain(a);
      const open = JSON.stringify(await ok('list_tickets', { projectId, filter: filters.savedFilters[0]!.filter }));
      expect(open).toContain(c);
      expect(open).not.toContain(a); // CONTROL: the filter filtered (a is closed)

      // 6. Assign by email: the preview first (nobody added), then the confirmation.
      const guestRows = async (userId: string) =>
        (await db.pool.query(`SELECT 1 FROM proj_memberships WHERE workspace_id = $1 AND user_id = $2`, [ws, userId])).rowCount;
      const previewA = (await ok('assign_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: t1, assignee_email: 'sweep2-out@example.test' })) as { preview: boolean; wouldChange: string; person: { userId: string }; joinsAsGuest: boolean };
      expect(previewA).toMatchObject({ preview: true, joinsAsGuest: true, person: { userId: outsider.userId } });
      expect(previewA.wouldChange).toContain('WILL BE ADDED AS A GUEST');
      expect(await guestRows(outsider.userId)).toBe(0);
      await ok('assign_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: t1, assignee_email: 'sweep2-out@example.test', confirm_assignee: previewA.person.userId });
      expect(await guestRows(outsider.userId)).toBe(1);
      expect(((await ok('get_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: t1 })) as { ticket: { assignee_id: string } }).ticket.assignee_id).toBe(outsider.userId);

      // 7. Create by email: the preview creates nothing; the confirmation creates it assigned.
      const countTickets = async () => Number((await db.pool.query(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM proj_tickets WHERE project_id = $1`, [projectId])).rows[0].n);
      const tickets = await countTickets();
      const previewC = (await ok('create_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, projectId, typeId: 'task', title: 'Born assigned', assignee_email: 'sweep2-second@example.test' })) as { preview: boolean; person: { userId: string } };
      expect(previewC.preview).toBe(true);
      expect(await countTickets()).toBe(tickets);
      const born = (await ok('create_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, projectId, typeId: 'task', title: 'Born assigned', assignee_email: 'sweep2-second@example.test', confirm_assignee: previewC.person.userId }))['ticketId'] as string;
      expect(await countTickets()).toBe(tickets + 1);
      expect(((await ok('get_ticket', { workspaceId: ws, ticketId: born })) as { ticket: { assignee_id: string } }).ticket.assignee_id).toBe(second.userId);
    });
  });
  ```

  Kills:
  - 14.659: none, guards existing behaviour (T3-T8's tools through the hosted door, end to end; T9 adds no tool code). Non-vacuous: (a) T3's `get_ticket` without the notes read → step 1's `[note]` fails while the bare-ticket control stays green; (b) T5's `close_tickets` commits on the first call (no preview) → `closedEvents()` is no longer `before` after the preview; (c) T7's `assign_ticket` grants the guest membership on the preview call → `guestRows(outsider)` is 1, not 0, before the confirmation. The no-op leg checks the reply only; 14.629 pins that it sends no PUT. With the unfiltered precondition, `list_tickets` dropping `filter` fails the step-5 control.

## Self-review (writing-plans Self-Review, done 2026-10-03 against the spec)

**1. Spec coverage**

| Spec | Tasks |
|---|---|
| §1 what exists today | read again at the tip; line drift noted in the gaps below |
| §2 decisions (S2-1 ... S2-9) | each is carried by the task that owns it; the points where the spec is silent or inconsistent are the plan's own decisions in Global Constraints ("Decisions this plan made") |
| §3 `get_ticket` notes (GT-1 ... GT-6) | T3, 14.605-14.609 |
| §4 `restore_note_version` (RV-1 ... RV-7) | T4, 14.610-14.616 |
| §5 `close_tickets` (CT-1 ... CT-11), the by-index reader, the preview | T2 (reader), T5, 14.617-14.626 |
| §6.1-6.5 notification preferences (NP-1 ... NP-8) | T6, 14.627-14.633 |
| §6.6 `list_saved_filters` (SF-1 ... SF-4) | T6, 14.634-14.637 |
| §7 `assign_ticket` by email (AE-1 ... AE-17) | T7, 14.638-14.652 |
| §7.7 `create_ticket` by email (CE-1 ... CE-4) | T8, 14.653-14.656 (and the parametrised T7 cases, flipped in T8) |
| §8 plumbing PL-1 ... PL-6 | T2 (PL-1 ... PL-5), T9 (PL-6 pins; each of T4-T6 also pins its own tools) |
| §9 Rule D2 tool by tool | no app screen changes; the two short rows (preference write unattributed, guest grant `origin: 'server'`) are in the catalogue text of 14.628 and 14.642 |
| §10 out of scope | nothing in the plan touches a route, a migration or an event type; Task 1 changes no server code |
| §12.1 case block | 14.600-14.659, all 60 allocated across T2-T9 (T1 mints none) |
| §12.3 documents | Features.md and `test-cases/14-mcp-server.md` per task, manual §16 and README in T9 |
| §13 O1 (lookup rate limit) | T1, measured first, owner decision handed off |
| §13 planner defaults | carried by the owning tasks; recorded in Global Constraints |

Gaps found in the spec or the code, all handled in the tasks and named in the HANDOFF of the task concerned:

- **`confirm_count` is not `.int()`.** Spec §5.3 types it as an integer; CT-9 requires `3.5` to answer a preview, and zod would refuse it before the handler ran. T5 types it `string | number` and compares text.
- **A lookup miss is a thrown error, not an answer.** Spec §7.3 says the miss sentence is "answered"; every other refusal in the package throws and the hosted door turns a throw into `isError`. T7 throws the sentence (14.645).
- **AE-10 and AE-11 disagree for a stranger on `assign_ticket`.** The ticket is read first (AE-11), so a stranger is told `ticket not found or not visible to you`, and only a guest who can read the ticket reaches the lookup and is told `workspace not found or not visible to you`. On `create_ticket` there is no ticket read, so a stranger gets the workspace sentence. 14.646 pins both.
- **Call 2 of `assign_ticket` reads the ticket only to build a fresh preview.** Spec §3 (the call list) puts a ticket read in call 2; T7 reads it only on the preview fallback so a ticket that went away between the calls yields the server's own refusal, not a second read.
- **`refused` in `close_tickets` counts every row not closed** (refused AND invalid), because the spec names two counts and three row statuses.
- **`close_ticket`'s lines drifted** to `ticket-tools.ts:108-120` (the spec says `:105-117`); the "sixty-three" prose is at `Features.md:3403` and `:3405` on the tip (find it with `rg -n sixty-three Features.md`).
- **The README gap.** `packages/mcp-server/README.md:66-70` omits all 39 MCP-everything tools. T9 documents this plan's tools and reports the older gap.
- **Existing tests change.** `get_ticket`'s 14.66 and 14.456 fakes need the notes route (T3), and `Session` literals gain `put` (T2: `NO_TRANSFER`, `NO_CLIENT_TRANSFER`). The 14.88 sweep drives every new handler.

**2. Placeholders.** Searched for TBD, TODO, "appropriate error handling", "similar to Task", "as above" and "write tests for": none. Where a step says "read X at your base first" it names the file and the line where the shape lives. The assumptions a lane must verify against the real server are written as explicit checks with a stated consequence, not left open:

- the `kind` key on `list_notifications` rows (T6);
- whether an epic reaches `done` through `doing` (T5);
- whether the owner may grant the `guest` role directly with `membership.granted` (T7's `email-world`);
- whether a workspace member may create in a project they are not on (T8, 14.656's second arm, with its stated fallback);
- the number of saved note versions after create and update (T9);
- the snake_case `assignee_id` key on `get_ticket` (T9).

**3. Type consistency.** These names are used identically across tasks:

- `Session.put`, `GisikClient.put`, `putJson`, `postJson`, `postOutsider`, `resultError`, `rateLimitedError`, `EventResult`, `submitBatchByIndex` (T2, used by T5-T8);
- `confirmOrPreview(expected, given, describe, param = 'confirm_name')`, with `'confirm_count'` in T5 and `'confirm_assignee'` in T7 (via `resolveAssignee`);
- `recordingSession`, `eventBatches`, `Recorded` (T2); `Actor`, `event`, `submitAs`, `mustCommit`, `grantRole`, `eventCount`, `noteBody` (T2's `sweep2.ts`);
- `EMAIL_FIELDS`, `LookedUpPerson`, `resolveAssignee`, `submitForAssignee`, `guestSentence` (T7, used by T8);
- `ROWS` / `HROWS` (T7 creates them with the `assign_ticket` row; T8 adds the `create_ticket` row), `buildEmailWorld` / `EmailWorld` / `Person` (T7);
- `TICKET_NOT_FOUND`, `VERSION_NOT_FOUND`, `DELETED_NOTE`, `MAX_TICKET_NOTES` (one definition each, imported where reused).

**4. Review Focus.** Each line has its case in the owning task: preview that wrote something 14.624 / 14.639 / 14.654; an almost-right confirm 14.624 / 14.643; an existing guest 14.652; a refused restore 14.615; the shared network key 14.648 with T1's measurement.

## Execution handoff

Execution: the gisik-nokma lanes (one task = one session), per the planner. The orchestrator dispatches each task to a lane as one fresh session, with reviewers and `ctl gate <sha> --base release/workflow-graph`, landing into `release/workflow-graph`; every branch is `task/<slug>` cut from `origin/release/workflow-graph`, never `main`.

- **Order:** T1 (its O1 line goes to the owner; per spec O1 "Nothing in this spec blocks on it", so the series does not wait: if the owner later rules a tighter cap or a bucket, that is a new task) → T2 → T3 and T7 in parallel → T8 after T7 → T4, T5, T6 one after another (they share the registry hunk) → T9 last. T3-T8 each need T2's merge.
- **Pointers:** no UI task, no `frontend-design`. T7 and T9 need the app-core build for their server files; T4-T6 pins must be re-read from the tip, never copied from this plan.
- **Reviewer focus:** the Review Focus lines, and for every task the three questions: did a preview write anything (read the recorded requests and the ledger, not the reply), does each control sit in the same `it`, and does the count in a pin come from the tip.
- I recommend **subagent-driven**: nine tasks with hard interface hand-offs (T2's helpers feed T3-T8, T7's module feeds T8), and a shipped mistake here either adds a stranger to a workspace by accident or closes tickets on a misread count.
